~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs;

/**
 * The COMPLETE nine-step plan, computed over the virtual state before anything
 * is written (plan gate r2, BLOCKER B2-1).
 *
 * A dry run renders it and stops. An apply executes it. Both paths produced it
 * with the same code, which is what makes the dry run a preview rather than a
 * different computation.
 */
final readonly class SyncPlan
{
    /**
     * @param  list<array{from: string, to: string}>                                       $renames        step 1
     * @param  list<string>                                                                $creates        step 2
     * @param  list<array{roleId: int, templateKey: string, customised: bool}>             $adoptions      step 4
     * @param  list<array{templateKey: string, templateVersion: int, writesMarker: bool}>  $roleCreations  step 5
     * @param  list<PlannedTemplateDelta>                                                  $templateDeltas step 6
     * @param  list<PlannedReplacement>                                                    $replacements   step 7
     * @param  array{target: list<string>}|null                                            $admin          step 8, null when admin already equals activeKeys()
     */
    public function __construct(
        public PermissionSyncResult $result,
        public array $renames = [],
        public array $creates = [],
        public array $adoptions = [],
        public array $roleCreations = [],
        public array $templateDeltas = [],
        public array $replacements = [],
        public ?array $admin = null,
        public bool $perTenant = true,
    ) {}

    /**
     * A non-green plan carries its verdict and NO work, so nothing can execute
     * it even by mistake. Its counters describe what was planned and refused.
     */
    public static function rejected(PermissionSyncResult $result): self
    {
        return new self($result);
    }
}
~~~

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs;

use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\PermissionSyncOutcome;

/**
 * The accumulating result of one sync. MUTABLE by design: the nine private step
 * methods each take and return it, so a reviewer can read step 4 without
 * reading step 6.
 *
 * TEN mutations (rows this run WROTE — all zero on a re-run, always) and TWO
 * gauges (state this run OBSERVED — unconstrained on a re-run). Nothing may
 * assert a gauge is zero.
 */
final class PermissionSyncResult
{
    public int $renamed = 0;
    public int $created = 0;
    public int $adoptedPristine = 0;
    public int $adoptedCustomised = 0;
    public int $rolesCreated = 0;
    public int $templatesApplied = 0;
    public int $templatesRemoved = 0;
    public int $replacementsGranted = 0;
    public int $adminChanges = 0;
    public int $markerWritten = 0;

    public int $orphans = 0;
    public int $templatesSkippedCustomised = 0;

    public PermissionSyncOutcome $outcome = PermissionSyncOutcome::AlreadyCurrent;
    public string $reason = 'already_current';

    /** @var list<string> per-role adoption/skip/delta log, rendered by --dry-run and by the handback */
    public array $log = [];

    /** @var list<string> rename entries whose `from` and `to` are BOTH absent — the map can be trimmed */
    public array $staleRenames = [];

    /** The conjunction the ALREADY_CURRENT definition needs. Gauges are deliberately absent. */
    public function hasMutations(): bool
    {
        return $this->renamed + $this->created + $this->adoptedPristine + $this->adoptedCustomised
            + $this->rolesCreated + $this->templatesApplied + $this->templatesRemoved
            + $this->replacementsGranted + $this->adminChanges + $this->markerWritten > 0;
    }

    public function blocked(string $reason): self
    {
        $this->outcome = PermissionSyncOutcome::Blocked;
        $this->reason = $reason;

        return $this;
    }

    public function failed(string $reason): self
    {
        $this->outcome = PermissionSyncOutcome::Failed;
        $this->reason = $reason;

        return $this;
    }

    /** Settle the outcome from the counters. Never overrides a BLOCKED or FAILED verdict. */
    public function settle(): self
    {
        if ($this->outcome === PermissionSyncOutcome::Blocked || $this->outcome === PermissionSyncOutcome::Failed) {
            return $this;
        }
        $this->outcome = $this->hasMutations() ? PermissionSyncOutcome::Applied : PermissionSyncOutcome::AlreadyCurrent;
        $this->reason = $this->hasMutations() ? 'applied' : 'already_current';

        return $this;
    }

    /** ONE shape for both commands — 0b rev 2's markerLine(tenantId, write) fix, adopted. */
    public function markerLine(string $tenantId, bool $write): string
    {
        return sprintf(
            'PERMISSIONS-SYNC tenant=%s mode=%s outcome=%s '
            .'mutations: renamed=%d created=%d adopted_pristine=%d adopted_customised=%d '
            .'roles_created=%d templates_applied=%d templates_removed=%d '
            .'replacements_granted=%d admin_changes=%d marker_written=%d '
            .'gauges: orphans=%d templates_skipped_customised=%d reason=%s',
            $tenantId,
            $write ? 'APPLY' : 'DRY_RUN',
            $this->outcome->value,
            $this->renamed, $this->created, $this->adoptedPristine, $this->adoptedCustomised,
            $this->rolesCreated, $this->templatesApplied, $this->templatesRemoved,
            $this->replacementsGranted, $this->adminChanges, $this->markerWritten,
            $this->orphans, $this->templatesSkippedCustomised,
            $this->reason,
        );
    }
}
~~~

### Step 2 — the service: one planner, one executor

> **`Clock` is the spec's name, and the repository has no such abstraction today** (`grep -i clock apps/api/app` returns only `ClockAnomalyDetector`). Use the repository's own time source rather than inventing an interface for one caller: inject `Illuminate\Support\DateFactory` (Laravel's `Date` facade backing, which `Carbon::setTestNow()` already controls in tests) and read `->now()`. If a `Clock` contract lands later, this is a one-line swap. **Record the substitution in the handback** — it is a deliberate deviation from the spec's signature, and a gate will ask.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs\PermissionSyncResult;
use App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs\PlannedReplacement;
use App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs\PlannedTemplateDelta;
use App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs\SyncPlan;
use App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs\VirtualRole;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Authorization\LegacyRoleBaseline;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Authorization\PermissionRenameMap;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Authorization\TemplateDeltaApplier;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\SystemRoleTemplate;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Events\RoleSyncedV1;
use App\Modules\Identity\Infrastructure\Locks\PermissionWriteLock;
use App\Modules\Identity\Infrastructure\Repositories\RoleMarkerRepository;
use App\Shared\Domain\Authorization\PermissionRegistry;
use DomainException;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Events\Dispatcher;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder as EloquentBuilder;
use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
use Illuminate\Support\DateFactory;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;
use Throwable;

/**
 * The catalogue sync. ONE public method, TWO phases, one transaction, one lock —
 * wave 0b's PermissionWriteLock, unchanged.
 *
 * PHASE 1 PLANS over a VIRTUAL STATE and writes nothing. PHASE 2 executes the
 * plan, and only when the caller asked for a write AND the plan is green. A dry
 * run is phase 1 alone; it is therefore the SAME computation as an apply rather
 * than a differently-shaped one (plan gate r2, BLOCKER B2-1).
 *
 * EVERY non-green outcome leaves the transaction by throwing SyncRollback, so a
 * BLOCKED or FAILED apply commits nothing at all.
 *
 * ADOPTION (step 4) IS PLANNED BEFORE ANY GRANT WRITE IS PLANNED. Running a
 * role-mutating step first is what let an earlier revision re-add a grant a
 * tenant had removed, making a customised role compare equal and be adopted
 * pristine.
 *
 * EVERY GRANT SET THE PLANNER COMPARES IS CANONICAL — canonicalise() on the way
 * into the virtual projection, on the template/admin targets, and on both sides
 * of the executor's divergence check. Step 1 renames the permission row IN PLACE
 * at execution time, so the applier re-reads post-rename names; a planner
 * comparing pre-rename names would compute a delta the applier does not, and
 * every ordinary legacy tenant would roll back with plan_divergence after a
 * green dry run (plan gate r3, BLOCKER B3-2).
 *
 * This class references NO symbol from lane/w-lot-a-1a — not
 * LotActionPermissionDelta, not LotActionPermissionActivation, not any
 * TemplateMigration* class. PermissionSyncServiceTest::sync_references_no_lot_action_symbol
 * asserts it statically.
 */
final readonly class PermissionSyncService
{
    private const GUARD = 'sanctum';

    private const MARKER_SOURCE = 'w-lot-a-1a';

    /**
     * The reserved scope token compatibility mode runs under (plan gate r3,
     * B3-6). It is NEVER a tenant id: it is the marker's `tenant=` token, the
     * advisory-lock subject for the one shared roles table, and it is what tells
     * this service to leave the Spatie permissions team ALONE so the NULL-team
     * rows are the ones step 4 classifies. A UUID could never collide with it.
     */
    public const COMPAT_SCOPE = 'compat';

    public function __construct(
        private PermissionRegistry $registry,
        private PermissionRegistrar $permissionRegistrar,
        private PermissionRenameMap $renameMap,
        private LegacyRoleBaseline $legacyBaseline,
        private RoleMarkerRepository $roleMarkers,
        private PermissionWriteLock $writeLock,
        private TemplateDeltaApplier $applier,
        private Dispatcher $events,
        private DateFactory $dates,
    ) {}

    public function sync(string $tenantId, bool $write): PermissionSyncResult
    {
        // COMPATIBILITY MODE sets NO team (plan gate r3, B3-6): the shared roles
        // table's rows are NULL-team, and setting a team id there would hide
        // exactly the roles step 4 exists to classify. $tenantId is COMPAT_SCOPE
        // on that path and is used only as the lock subject and the marker token.
        $perTenant = (bool) config('tenancy_resolver.db_per_tenant');
        $previousTeamId = $this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId();
        if ($perTenant) {
            $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($tenantId);
        }

        try {
            return DB::transaction(function () use ($tenantId, $write, $perTenant): PermissionSyncResult {
                // The lock is the FIRST statement in the transaction.
                $this->writeLock->acquire($tenantId);

                // ---- PHASE 1: PLAN. Reads only. Identical on both paths. ----
                $plan = $this->plan($tenantId);
                $result = $plan->result;

                // ---- PHASE 2: EXECUTE. Writing path, green plan, nothing else.
                if ($write && $result->outcome->isGreen()) {
                    $this->execute($plan, $tenantId);

                    if ($plan->perTenant) {
                        $this->verifyAdmin($result);
                    }

                    if ($result->outcome->isGreen()) {
                        $this->registerCacheFlushAfterCommit($perTenant ? $tenantId : null);
                    }
                }

                // ---- PHASE 3: EXIT. A dry run and EVERY non-green apply leave
                // by ROLLBACK, never by ordinary return.
                if (! $write || ! $result->outcome->isGreen()) {
                    throw new SyncRollback($result);
                }

                return $result;
            });
        } catch (SyncRollback $rollback) {
            return $rollback->result;
        } catch (DomainException $blocked) {
            return (new PermissionSyncResult)->blocked($blocked->getMessage());
        } catch (Throwable $failure) {
            return (new PermissionSyncResult)->failed('exception:'.$failure->getMessage());
        } finally {
            if ($perTenant) {
                $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($previousTeamId);
            }
        }
    }

    // =====================================================================
    // PHASE 1 — the planner. Nine steps over a virtual state. ZERO writes.
    // =====================================================================

    private function plan(string $tenantId): SyncPlan
    {
        $result = new PermissionSyncResult;
        $perTenant = (bool) config('tenancy_resolver.db_per_tenant');

        // ---- the VIRTUAL permission-name set --------------------------------
        /** @var list<string> $names */
        $names = DB::table('permissions')->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)->pluck('name')->all();
        $names = array_values(array_unique($names));

        // ---- step 1: rename -------------------------------------------------
        /** @var list<array{from: string, to: string}> $renames */
        $renames = [];
        foreach ($this->renameMap->entries() as $entry) {
            $fromExists = in_array($entry['from'], $names, true);
            $toExists = in_array($entry['to'], $names, true);

            if ($fromExists && $toExists) {
                // A human must decide which grants win. BLOCKED, and the plan is empty.
                return SyncPlan::rejected($result->blocked('rename_target_exists:'.$entry['from'].'->'.$entry['to']));
            }
            if (! $fromExists) {
                if (! $toExists) {
                    $result->staleRenames[] = $entry['from'].'->'.$entry['to'];
                }

                continue;
            }

            $renames[] = $entry;
            $result->renamed++;
            $result->log[] = 'renamed '.$entry['from'].' -> '.$entry['to'];

            // VIRTUAL: the row now carries the target name. IN PLACE at execution
            // time, so the primary key never changes and no grant pivot is rewritten.
            $names = array_values(array_map(
                static fn (string $name): string => $name === $entry['from'] ? $entry['to'] : $name,
                $names,
            ));
        }

        // ---- step 2: create -------------------------------------------------
        /** @var list<string> $creates */
        $creates = [];
        foreach ($this->registry->keys() as $key) {   // deprecated INCLUDED
            if (in_array($key, $names, true)) {
                continue;
            }
            $creates[] = $key;
            $result->created++;
            $names[] = $key;                          // VIRTUAL
        }

        // ---- step 3: orphans (a READ, over the post-step-2 virtual set) -----
        $orphans = array_values(array_diff($names, $this->registry->keys()));
        sort($orphans);
        $result->orphans = count($orphans);           // a GAUGE: reported, never deleted, never asserted zero
        foreach ($orphans as $orphan) {
            $result->log[] = 'orphan '.$orphan;
        }

        // ---- the VIRTUAL role state ----------------------------------------
        // ONE locked read, in wave 0b's row order, and everything after it reads
        // the projection. The refusal below is what makes keying the projection
        // by role NAME sound: after wave 1, template_key and name are equal by
        // construction on every template-linked role, and a row where they are
        // not is a state this service must not guess about.
        $this->refuseTemplateKeyNameMismatch();
        $state = $this->virtualiseRoles($this->resolveTemplateRoles($tenantId));

        // ---- step 4: adopt --------------------------------------------------
        /** @var list<array{roleId: int, templateKey: string, customised: bool}> $adoptions */
        $adoptions = [];
        foreach (SystemRoleTemplate::cases() as $template) {
            $virtual = $state[$template->value] ?? null;
            if ($virtual === null || $virtual->templateKey !== null) {
                continue;   // adoption is ONE-WAY and ONCE
            }

            $pristine = $this->legacyBaseline->matchesVersion0($template->value, $virtual->grants);

            $adoptions[] = [
                'roleId' => $virtual->requireId(),
                'templateKey' => $template->value,
                'customised' => ! $pristine,
            ];
            $pristine ? $result->adoptedPristine++ : $result->adoptedCustomised++;
            $result->log[] = sprintf('adopt %s as %s', $template->value, $pristine ? 'pristine' : 'customised');

            $state[$template->value] = $virtual->adopted($template->value, customised: ! $pristine);   // VIRTUAL
        }

        if (! $perTenant) {
            // Compatibility mode (`permissions:sync --compat`): steps 5-8 would
            // provision one tenant's roles for everybody in a shared table.
            // Stated, never silently degraded — and reachable, which is the
            // half rev 3 did not have (plan gate r3, B3-6).
            $result->log[] = 'compatibility_mode: steps 5-8 skipped (shared roles table)';
            $result->settle();

            return new SyncPlan(
                result: $result,
                renames: $renames,
                creates: $creates,
                adoptions: $adoptions,
                perTenant: false,
            );
        }

        $version = $this->registry->version();

        // ---- step 5: create missing template roles --------------------------
        $marked = $this->roleMarkers->tenantCarriesMarker(self::MARKER_SOURCE);
        /** @var list<array{templateKey: string, templateVersion: int, writesMarker: bool}> $roleCreations */
        $roleCreations = [];
        foreach (SystemRoleTemplate::cases() as $template) {
            if (isset($state[$template->value])) {
                continue;
            }

            // The ONE case in which provisioning_source is written, ever.
            $writesMarker = $template === SystemRoleTemplate::GeneralManager && ! $marked;

            $roleCreations[] = [
                'templateKey' => $template->value,
                'templateVersion' => $version,
                'writesMarker' => $writesMarker,
            ];
            $result->rolesCreated++;
            $result->log[] = 'create role '.$template->value;
            if ($writesMarker) {
                $result->markerWritten = 1;
                $result->log[] = 'marker written on general_manager';
            }

            // VIRTUAL: it now exists, uncustomised, at the registry version, with
            // NO grants — so step 6 plans its full grant delta and step 8 finds
            // it. This is the line rev 2 did not have, and B2-1 is its absence.
            $state[$template->value] = VirtualRole::planned($template->value, $version);
        }

        // ---- step 6: template deltas ----------------------------------------
        $templateGrants = $this->registry->templateGrants();
        /** @var list<PlannedTemplateDelta> $templateDeltas */
        $templateDeltas = [];
        foreach (SystemRoleTemplate::declared() as $template) {   // non-admin only
            $virtual = $state[$template->value] ?? null;
            if ($virtual === null) {
                continue;   // unreachable after step 5; kept as a guard, not as a hope
            }
            if ($virtual->customised) {
                $result->templatesSkippedCustomised++;   // a GAUGE
                $result->log[] = 'skip customised '.$template->value;

                continue;
            }

            // BOTH sides are canonical: the projection was canonicalised on the
            // way in, and the template target is canonicalised here so the two
            // are comparable as sets even if a manifest ever declared a rename
            // source (no_manifest_declares_a_rename_source_key forbids it, and
            // this line makes the comparison independent of that test). Plan
            // gate r3, B3-2.
            /** @var list<string> $target */
            $target = $this->renameMap->canonicalise($templateGrants[$template->value] ?? []);
            $added = array_values(array_diff($target, $virtual->grants));
            $removed = array_values(array_diff($virtual->grants, $target));
            $versionChanged = $virtual->templateVersion !== $version;

            if ($added === [] && $removed === [] && ! $versionChanged) {
                continue;
            }

            $templateDeltas[] = new PlannedTemplateDelta(
                roleId: $virtual->id,
                roleName: $template->value,
                templateKey: $template->value,
                added: $added,
                removed: $removed,
                fromVersion: $virtual->templateVersion,
                toVersion: $version,
                versionChanged: $versionChanged,
            );

            if ($added !== []) {
                $result->templatesApplied++;
            }
            if ($removed !== []) {
                $result->templatesRemoved++;
            }
            if ($added === [] && $removed === [] && $versionChanged) {
                // A version-only advance is still a WRITE and must be counted, or
                // ALREADY_CURRENT would claim zero mutations over one row.
                $result->templatesApplied++;
            }
            if ($added !== [] || $removed !== []) {
                $result->log[] = sprintf('delta %s +%d -%d', $template->value, count($added), count($removed));
            }

            $state[$template->value] = $virtual->withGrants($target, $version);   // VIRTUAL
        }

        // ---- step 7: deprecation replacement grants -------------------------
        /** @var list<PlannedReplacement> $replacements */
        $replacements = [];
        foreach ($this->registry->definitions() as $definition) {
            if (! $definition->deprecated || $definition->replacedBy === null) {
                continue;   // all 22 of wave 1's deprecations are replacedBy: null
            }

            foreach ($this->holdersOf($definition->key) as $holder) {
                // A template role's grant set may already have been changed by
                // step 6 in the virtual state; a CUSTOM role's has not.
                $effective = isset($state[$holder['name']]) ? $state[$holder['name']]->grants : $holder['grants'];
                if (in_array($definition->replacedBy, $effective, true)) {
                    continue;
                }

                $replacements[] = new PlannedReplacement(
                    roleId: $holder['id'],
                    roleName: $holder['name'],
                    templateKey: $holder['templateKey'],
                    templateVersion: $holder['templateVersion'],
                    deprecatedKey: $definition->key,
                    replacementKey: $definition->replacedBy,
                );
                $result->replacementsGranted++;
                $result->log[] = sprintf('replacement %s -> %s on %s', $definition->key, $definition->replacedBy, $holder['name']);

                if (isset($state[$holder['name']])) {
                    $state[$holder['name']] = $state[$holder['name']]->plus($definition->replacedBy);   // VIRTUAL
                }
            }
        }

        // ---- step 8: admin ---------------------------------------------------
        $adminVirtual = $state[SystemRoleTemplate::Admin->value] ?? null;
        if ($adminVirtual === null) {
            // The PLANNING invariant. Step 5 plans an admin whenever one is
            // missing, so this fires only if SystemRoleTemplate::cases() stops
            // containing Admin. The REACHABLE refusal is verifyAdmin(), below.
            return SyncPlan::rejected($result->failed('admin_role_missing'));
        }

        $adminTarget = $this->renameMap->canonicalise($this->registry->activeKeys());
        $adminAdded = array_values(array_diff($adminTarget, $adminVirtual->grants));
        $adminRemoved = array_values(array_diff($adminVirtual->grants, $adminTarget));

        /** @var array{target: list<string>}|null $admin */
        $admin = null;
        if ($adminAdded !== [] || $adminRemoved !== []) {
            $admin = ['target' => $adminTarget];
            $result->adminChanges = count($adminAdded) + count($adminRemoved);   // INCLUDING removals
            $result->log[] = sprintf('admin +%d -%d', count($adminAdded), count($adminRemoved));
            $state[SystemRoleTemplate::Admin->value] = $adminVirtual->withGrants($adminTarget, $adminVirtual->templateVersion);
        }

        $result->settle();

        return new SyncPlan(
            result: $result,
            renames: $renames,
            creates: $creates,
            adoptions: $adoptions,
            roleCreations: $roleCreations,
            templateDeltas: $templateDeltas,
            replacements: $replacements,
            admin: $admin,
        );
    }

    // =====================================================================
    // PHASE 2 — the executor. It performs the plan and nothing else.
    // =====================================================================

    private function execute(SyncPlan $plan, string $tenantId): void
    {
        $version = $this->registry->version();
        $team = (string) config('permission.column_names.team_foreign_key');

        // step 1 — IN PLACE: the primary key never changes, so every
        // role_has_permissions and model_has_permissions row survives untouched.
        foreach ($plan->renames as $rename) {
            DB::table('permissions')
                ->where('name', $rename['from'])
                ->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)
                ->update(['name' => $rename['to']]);
        }

        // step 2 — create(), never firstOrCreate: the PLAN already knows which
        // branch each key takes, which is what made `created=` countable.
        foreach ($plan->creates as $key) {
            Permission::create(['name' => $key, 'guard_name' => self::GUARD]);
        }

        // step 3 writes nothing.

        // step 4 — adoption.
        foreach ($plan->adoptions as $adoption) {
            DB::table('roles')->where('id', $adoption['roleId'])->update([
                'is_system' => true,
                'template_key' => $adoption['templateKey'],
                'template_version' => 0,
                'customised_at' => $adoption['customised'] ? $this->dates->now() : null,
            ]);
        }

        // step 5 — missing template roles.
        /** @var array<string, Role> $created */
        $created = [];
        foreach ($plan->roleCreations as $creation) {
            $attributes = [
                'name' => $creation['templateKey'],
                'guard_name' => self::GUARD,
                $team => $tenantId,
                'is_system' => true,
                'template_key' => $creation['templateKey'],
                'template_version' => $creation['templateVersion'],
                'customised_at' => null,
            ];
            if ($creation['writesMarker']) {
                // Every clause of the lane's CHECK holds by construction here:
                // sync always sets a tenant id, always this name and this guard.
                $attributes['provisioning_source'] = self::MARKER_SOURCE;
            }

            $created[$creation['templateKey']] = Role::create($attributes);
        }

        // step 6 — the ONE applier, once per planned delta, verified against the plan.
        foreach ($plan->templateDeltas as $planned) {
            $role = $created[$planned->templateKey] ?? $this->lockTemplateRole($planned->templateKey);
            if ($role === null) {
                throw new SyncRollback($plan->result->failed('plan_divergence:role_gone:'.$planned->templateKey));
            }

            $delta = $this->applier->applyTo($role, $version);

            // A planner whose numbers can disagree with the writer's is worse
            // than no planner. Any disagreement rolls the whole run back.
            //
            // The comparison is over CANONICAL SETS, not over the two lists as
            // produced (plan gate r3, B3-2). The applier re-reads the role AFTER
            // step 1 renamed the permission rows in place, so its names are
            // already canonical; canonicalising both sides makes the assertion
            // independent of that ordering AND of the diff order, which
            // array_diff inherits from its first argument.
            if ($this->renameMap->canonicalise($delta->added) !== $this->renameMap->canonicalise($planned->added)
                || $this->renameMap->canonicalise($delta->removed) !== $this->renameMap->canonicalise($planned->removed)
                || $delta->versionChanged !== $planned->versionChanged) {
                throw new SyncRollback($plan->result->failed('plan_divergence:'.$planned->templateKey));
            }

            if ($delta->added !== [] || $delta->removed !== []) {
                $this->events->dispatch(new RoleSyncedV1(
                    roleId: (int) $role->getKey(),
                    roleName: $planned->roleName,
                    templateKey: $planned->templateKey,
                    added: $delta->added,
                    removed: $delta->removed,
                    cause: 'template_delta',
                    fromTemplateVersion: $delta->fromVersion,
                    toTemplateVersion: $delta->toVersion,
                    tenantId: $tenantId,
                    actorUserId: null,
                    occurredAtIso: $this->dates->now()->toIso8601String(),
                ));
            }
        }

        // step 7 — the one case in which sync writes a CUSTOM role, audited.
        foreach ($plan->replacements as $planned) {
            $role = Role::query()->whereKey($planned->roleId)->lockForUpdate()->first();
            if ($role === null) {
                throw new SyncRollback($plan->result->failed('plan_divergence:holder_gone:'.$planned->roleName));
            }

            $role->givePermissionTo($planned->replacementKey);
            $this->events->dispatch(new RoleSyncedV1(
                roleId: $planned->roleId,
                roleName: $planned->roleName,
                templateKey: $planned->templateKey,
                added: [$planned->replacementKey],
                removed: [],
                cause: 'deprecation_replacement',
                fromTemplateVersion: $planned->templateVersion,
                toTemplateVersion: $planned->templateVersion,
                tenantId: $tenantId,
                actorUserId: null,
                occurredAtIso: $this->dates->now()->toIso8601String(),
            ));
        }

        // step 8 — the one place a full replace is correct.
        if ($plan->admin !== null) {
            $admin = $created[SystemRoleTemplate::Admin->value] ?? $this->lockTemplateRole(SystemRoleTemplate::Admin->value);
            if ($admin === null) {
                throw new SyncRollback($plan->result->failed('admin_role_missing'));
            }

            $admin->syncPermissions($plan->admin['target']);
        }
    }

    /**
     * The REACHABLE fail-closed refusal (plan gate r2, B2-1). Run inside the
     * transaction, after execute(), under the same lock.
     *
     * A tenant whose routes are gated on keys nobody holds 403s every caller
     * including the operator, so an apply that cannot leave `admin` equal to
     * activeKeys() must leave the database untouched instead. Setting the
     * outcome non-green here makes PHASE 3 throw SyncRollback, which rolls back
     * steps 1-8 together.
     */
    private function verifyAdmin(PermissionSyncResult $result): void
    {
        $admin = $this->lockTemplateRole(SystemRoleTemplate::Admin->value);
        if ($admin === null) {
            $result->failed('admin_role_missing');

            return;
        }

        /** @var list<string> $held */
        $held = $admin->permissions()->toBase()->pluck('name')->all();
        $target = $this->registry->activeKeys();

        if (array_diff($target, $held) !== [] || array_diff($held, $target) !== []) {
            $result->failed('admin_not_current');
        }
    }

    /**
     * Registered INSIDE the transaction so it fires once the commit lands. A
     * `finally` inside the transaction callback would execute BEFORE Laravel
     * commits, leaving a window in which another request rebuilds and re-caches
     * the OLD snapshot.
     *
     * THE CALLBACK SETS ITS OWN TEAM AND RESTORES IT (plan gate r3, BLOCKER
     * B3-5). Rev 3 relied on `sync()`'s outer scope still holding the tenant's
     * team id when the callback ran, which is true only when this service opens
     * the ROOT transaction. Laravel executes after-commit callbacks only when
     * the root transaction reaches level zero —
     * `afterCommitCallbacksShouldBeExecuted($level) { return $level === 0; }` at
     * vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Database/DatabaseTransactionsManager.php:243-246,
     * called from commit() at :75-78 — and the compatibility REGISTRATION path
     * already wraps tenant initialization in an OUTER transaction
     * (dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/AuthController.php:362,
     * with initializeForNewRegistration() inside it at :488-492). On that path
     * the inner service transaction returns, sync()'s `finally` restores the
     * PREVIOUS team, and only later does the outer commit run the callback —
     * which would then flush the WRONG S-1 namespace and leave this tenant's
     * stale snapshot cached. Capturing the id by value and re-setting it inside
     * the callback makes the flush correct at any nesting depth.
     *
     * @param  string|null  $tenantId  the tenant whose S-1 namespace to flush;
     *                                 NULL in compatibility mode, where there is
     *                                 one global namespace and no team to set.
     */
    private function registerCacheFlushAfterCommit(?string $tenantId): void
    {
        DB::afterCommit(function () use ($tenantId): void {
            if ($tenantId === null) {
                $this->permissionRegistrar->forgetCachedPermissions();

                return;
            }

            $teamAtCallbackTime = $this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId();
            $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($tenantId);

            try {
                $this->permissionRegistrar->forgetCachedPermissions();
            } finally {
                // Restore whatever the surrounding request/job was using: an
                // after-commit callback runs on someone else's stack.
                $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($teamAtCallbackTime);
            }
        });
    }

    // ---- shared helpers -------------------------------------------------

    private function lockTemplateRole(string $templateKey): ?Role
    {
        return Role::query()
            ->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)
            ->where('template_key', $templateKey)
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->first();
    }

    /**
     * The virtual state is keyed by role NAME. On every role wave 1 touches,
     * `template_key` and `name` are equal by construction — adoption writes
     * `template_key = <name>`, step 5 creates with both equal, and a tenant
     * provisioned after wave 1 has both equal. A row where they differ is a
     * state this service must not guess about, so it refuses.
     *
     * @throws DomainException
     */
    private function refuseTemplateKeyNameMismatch(): void
    {
        $names = array_map(static fn (SystemRoleTemplate $t): string => $t->value, SystemRoleTemplate::cases());

        $mismatch = DB::table('roles')
            ->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)
            ->whereIn('template_key', $names)
            ->whereColumn('template_key', '!=', 'name')
            ->value('name');

        if ($mismatch !== null) {
            throw new DomainException('template_key_name_mismatch:'.(string) $mismatch);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Every virtual grant set is CANONICALISED as it enters the projection
     * (plan gate r3, BLOCKER B3-2). The rows this reads still carry their
     * PRE-rename spelling — step 1 renames the `permissions` row in place at
     * EXECUTION time, and the pivot follows the primary key — so a projection
     * built from the live names would make the planner compute
     * `+uom.update / -uom.edit` while the applier, re-reading after the rename,
     * computes nothing, and every ordinary legacy tenant would roll back with
     * `plan_divergence`. `PermissionRenameMap::canonicalise()` is 0b's
     * single-pass, deduplicated, sorted, order-free and idempotent map
     * (`2f172dd5e:docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-10-rbac-wave-0b.md:413-433`),
     * so it also supplies the sort this method used to do by hand.
     *
     * @param  Collection<string, Role>  $roles
     * @return array<string, VirtualRole>
     */
    private function virtualiseRoles(Collection $roles): array
    {
        /** @var array<string, VirtualRole> $state */
        $state = [];
        foreach ($roles as $name => $role) {
            $templateKey = $role->getAttribute('template_key');
            $templateVersion = $role->getAttribute('template_version');
            /** @var list<string> $held */
            $held = $role->permissions()->toBase()->pluck('name')->all();
            $grants = $this->renameMap->canonicalise($held);

            $state[(string) $name] = VirtualRole::existing(
                id: (int) $role->getKey(),
                name: (string) $name,
                templateKey: is_string($templateKey) && $templateKey !== '' ? $templateKey : null,
                templateVersion: is_int($templateVersion) ? $templateVersion : 0,
                customised: $role->getAttribute('customised_at') !== null,
                grants: array_values($grants),
            );
        }

        return $state;
    }

    /**
     * Every role holding a deprecated key, CUSTOM roles included — that is the
     * whole point of step 7.
     *
     * Its grant sets are CANONICALISED for the same reason virtualiseRoles()'s
     * are (plan gate r3, B3-2): the `in_array($definition->replacedBy, …)`
     * short-circuit below must not re-grant a successor a role already holds
     * under a pre-rename spelling.
     *
     * @return list<array{id: int, name: string, templateKey: string|null, templateVersion: int|null, grants: list<string>}>
     */
    private function holdersOf(string $key): array
    {
        $holders = Role::query()
            ->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)
            ->whereHas('permissions', static fn (EloquentBuilder $query): EloquentBuilder => $query->where('name', $key))
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->get();

        $rows = [];
        foreach ($holders as $role) {
            $templateKey = $role->getAttribute('template_key');
            $templateVersion = $role->getAttribute('template_version');
            /** @var list<string> $held */
            $held = $role->permissions()->toBase()->pluck('name')->all();

            $rows[] = [
                'id' => (int) $role->getKey(),
                'name' => (string) $role->getAttribute('name'),
                'templateKey' => is_string($templateKey) && $templateKey !== '' ? $templateKey : null,
                'templateVersion' => is_int($templateVersion) ? $templateVersion : null,
                'grants' => $this->renameMap->canonicalise($held),
            ];
        }

        return $rows;
    }

    /**
     * lane/w-lot-a-1a's resolveCatalogueRoles(), as wave 0b re-expressed it:
     * NULL-or-tenant team predicate, tenant_id PRESERVED as found, ordered name
     * then id, lockForUpdate, and both refusals. UNCHANGED in rev 3 — it is on
     * the r9 Preserve list, and it is now the ONLY read of `roles` the plan
     * makes.
     *
     * @return Collection<string, Role>
     */
    private function resolveTemplateRoles(string $tenantId): Collection
    {
        $team = (string) config('permission.column_names.team_foreign_key');
        $names = array_map(static fn (SystemRoleTemplate $t): string => $t->value, SystemRoleTemplate::cases());

        /** @var Collection<int, Role> $roles */
        $roles = Role::query()
            ->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)
            ->whereIn('name', $names)
            ->where(fn ($query) => $query->whereNull($team)->orWhere($team, $tenantId))
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->get();

        foreach ($roles->groupBy('name') as $name => $matches) {
            if ($name === SystemRoleTemplate::GeneralManager->value && $matches->contains(
                static fn (Role $role): bool => $role->getAttribute('provisioning_source') !== self::MARKER_SOURCE,
            )) {
                throw new DomainException('unmarked_general_manager_collision');
            }
            if ($matches->count() !== 1) {
                throw new DomainException('ambiguous_legacy_role_collision');
            }
        }

        return $roles->keyBy('name');
    }
}
~~~

### Step 3 — `permissions:sync`, which INITIALIZES the tenant it names

**`--tenant=` must switch the database connection, not merely label the output** (plan gate r2, BLOCKER **B2-2**). Under database-per-tenant, `PermissionSyncService` sets Spatie's team id and nothing else; it does **not** initialize Stancl tenancy. Rev 2's command resolved a central `Tenant` id and called the service directly, so an operator running `permissions:sync --tenant=<other>` from a container whose context was tenant A would have **written tenant A's roles while printing tenant B's id** — a cross-tenant data-safety defect, and the remediation command is exactly where an operator reaches for a tenant that is not the current one.

The repository already has one shape for this and the command copies it, rather than inventing a second:

| Concern | The existing code this mirrors |
|---|---|
| initialize before the work | `dev:apps/api/app/Console/TenantScopedCommand.php:323-332` — `if ($dbPerTenant) { tenancy()->initialize($tenant); $initialized = true; }` at `:327-330` |
| end it afterwards, guarded | `dev:apps/api/app/Console/TenantScopedCommand.php:342-343` — `if ($initialized && tenancy()->initialized) { tenancy()->end(); }` |
| the fail-closed resolver's own idiom | `dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Tenant/Application/Services/TenancyResolver.php:81-107` — `initializeIfProvisioned()`, with `tenancy()->initialize($tenant)` at `:105` |

Two rules the command follows from that:
- **it ends tenancy only if it started it FROM CENTRAL.** With no `--tenant`, the command runs in whatever context the caller established (the entrypoint, a queued job, a test) and ending that context would break the caller;
- **`tenancy()` is a first-party helper, not the `app()` escape hatch.** The Global Constraint forbids `app()`; `tenancy()` is what `TenantScopedCommand` and `TenancyResolver` both use, and there is no injectable façade for it in this tree.

> **And one rule wave 1 does NOT take from that mirror: a prior tenant is RESTORED, not ended** (plan gate r3, BLOCKER **B3-3**; orchestrator ruling **[R]**). Stancl's `initialize()` **already ends** whatever tenant was active before it switches — `if ($this->initialized) { $this->end(); }` at `/Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/apps/api/vendor/stancl/tenancy/src/Tenancy.php:48-50` — and `end()` sets `$this->tenant = null` at `:73`. So rev 3's `$initialized = true; … finally { if ($initialized && tenancy()->initialized) { tenancy()->end(); } }` **destroys** a pre-existing tenant-A context instead of restoring it: A is ended by `initialize(B)`, B is ended by the `finally`, and the process is left in **no** tenant context at all. That directly contradicts the promised oracle — *"after any of them `tenancy()->initialized` is back to what it was before the call, with the process still able to query tenant A"*. `TenantScopedCommand::forEachTenant` gets away with the same shape because it is a **fleet iterator that runs from central**; `permissions:sync --tenant=` is the **remediation** command an operator reaches for **from inside another tenant's context**, which is exactly the case the mirror never has. Rev 4 therefore **snapshots the previously active tenant object** before initializing, and in `finally` re-`initialize($previous)` when one existed, calling `end()` **only** when the command began from central. `SyncPermissionsTenantSelectorTest` asserts A is restored after `--tenant=B`.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services\PermissionSyncService;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Tenant;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

final class SyncPermissions extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'permissions:sync
        {--tenant= : Sync one tenant by id or slug; omit to sync the current tenant context}
        {--compat : COMPATIBILITY MODE ONLY. Run steps 1-4 once, globally, over the shared roles table from central context}
        {--dry-run : Compute and report the plan; write nothing}';

    protected $description = 'Apply the permission catalogue and the additive template delta to one tenant.';

    public function __construct(
        private readonly PermissionSyncService $sync,
        private readonly PermissionRegistrar $permissionRegistrar,
    ) {
        parent::__construct();
    }

    public function handle(): int
    {
        $write = ! (bool) $this->option('dry-run');
        $perTenant = (bool) config('tenancy_resolver.db_per_tenant');
        $compat = (bool) $this->option('compat');
        $selector = $this->option('tenant');
        $selector = is_string($selector) && $selector !== '' ? $selector : null;

        // ---- the four refusals, before any work -----------------------------
        if ($selector !== null && ! $perTenant) {
            $this->error('PERMISSIONS-SYNC outcome=BLOCKED reason=compatibility_mode_is_global');
            $this->line('In compatibility mode the roles table is shared, so --tenant cannot mean anything. Run with --compat instead.');

            return 2;   // BLOCKED, on the applying AND the dry-run path
        }

        if ($compat && $perTenant) {
            $this->error('PERMISSIONS-SYNC outcome=BLOCKED reason=compat_flag_in_db_per_tenant_mode');
            $this->line('--compat is the shared-roles-table path. Under database-per-tenant use --tenant=, or permissions:sync-fleet.');

            return 2;
        }

        if (! $perTenant && ! $compat) {
            // Without the flag a central Artisan process has no permissions team
            // and would exit `no_tenant_context` with nothing an operator could
            // do about it (plan gate r3, B3-6). Name the one command that works.
            $this->error('PERMISSIONS-SYNC outcome=BLOCKED reason=compat_requires_flag');
            $this->line('This deployment is in compatibility mode (tenancy_resolver.db_per_tenant=false). '
                .'Run `php artisan permissions:sync --compat` to apply steps 1-4 once, globally.');

            return 2;
        }

        // ---- resolve the tenant, by id OR slug ------------------------------
        $tenant = null;
        if ($selector !== null) {
            $tenant = Tenant::query()
                ->where('id', $selector)
                ->orWhere('slug', $selector)
                ->first();

            if ($tenant === null) {
                $this->error('PERMISSIONS-SYNC outcome=FAILED reason=tenant_not_found selector='.$selector);

                return 1;
            }
        }

        // In compatibility mode there is no tenant to name, so the marker's
        // scope token is the reserved COMPAT_SCOPE and the service leaves the
        // Spatie team id untouched — the roles it classifies are NULL-team rows.
        $tenantId = match (true) {
            $compat => PermissionSyncService::COMPAT_SCOPE,
            $tenant !== null => (string) $tenant->id,
            default => (string) ($this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId() ?? ''),
        };

        if ($tenantId === '') {
            $this->error('PERMISSIONS-SYNC outcome=FAILED reason=no_tenant_context');

            return 1;
        }

        // ---- INITIALIZE the tenant's database, and RESTORE the caller's ------
        // Rev 3 mirrored TenantScopedCommand::forEachTenant (dev:…:323-332,
        // 342-343). That mirror is a FLEET iterator running from central; this
        // is the REMEDIATION command, which an operator runs from inside another
        // tenant's context. Stancl's initialize() already ends the active tenant
        // (vendor/stancl/tenancy/src/Tenancy.php:48-50) and end() nulls it
        // (:73), so `finally { end(); }` would leave NO tenant active and
        // destroy tenant A (plan gate r3, B3-3).
        $previous = tenancy()->initialized ? tenant() : null;
        $startedFromCentral = $previous === null;

        try {
            if ($tenant !== null) {
                tenancy()->initialize($tenant);
            }

            $result = $this->sync->sync($tenantId, $write);
            $marker = $result->markerLine($tenantId, $write);

            $this->line($marker);
            Log::channel('stderr')->info($marker);
            foreach ($result->log as $line) {
                $this->line('  '.$line);
            }
            foreach ($result->staleRenames as $stale) {
                $this->warn('  stale rename entry (neither side present): '.$stale);
            }

            return $result->outcome->exitCode();
        } finally {
            if ($previous !== null) {
                // RESTORE, never end: initialize() is idempotent when the same
                // tenant is already active (Tenancy.php:43-45), so this is a
                // no-op when --tenant was omitted and a real switch-back when
                // it named a different tenant.
                tenancy()->initialize($previous);
            } elseif ($startedFromCentral && $tenant !== null && tenancy()->initialized) {
                // END only when this command started from central context.
                tenancy()->end();
            }
        }
    }
}
~~~

> **`permissions:sync-fleet` needs none of this.** Wave 0b's `TenantFleetRunner` owns selection, readiness and per-tenant tenancy initialization for the fleet path (0b's `run(array, string, Closure)`), and its own `runOne()` ends the tenant in a `finally` (`2f172dd5e:docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-10-rbac-wave-0b.md:1596-1599`); the closure it invokes is already running inside the tenant. That asymmetry is exactly why the per-tenant command has to do it itself — and why only the per-tenant command has a prior context worth restoring.

#### Compatibility mode — the non-production topology, with ONE executable path

**`permissions:sync --compat` is that path** (plan gate r3, BLOCKER **B3-6**; orchestrator ruling **[R]**). Rev 3 promised steps 1–4 would run "once globally" in compatibility mode and left **no way to invoke them**: `--tenant=` is refused, a central Artisan process has no Spatie permissions team so the bare command exits `no_tenant_context`, and the fleet path cannot help because `TenantFleetRunner::runOne()` requires each tenant's **physical database to exist** (`2f172dd5e:docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-10-rbac-wave-0b.md:1578-1580`) while `TenancyResolver` treats an absent per-tenant database as **normal** in this mode (`dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Tenant/Application/Services/TenancyResolver.php:90-102`). The flag closes that hole with an explicit path rather than a silent degrade:

| Property | `--compat` |
|---|---|
| Topology | `config('tenancy_resolver.db_per_tenant') === false` **only**; the flag is refused (exit 2, `reason=compat_flag_in_db_per_tenant_mode`) under database-per-tenant |
| Context | **central**. It performs **no** `tenancy()->initialize()`, resolves **no** `Tenant` row, and consults **no** readiness predicate — there is nothing to be ready |
| Permissions team | **untouched**. `PermissionSyncService::sync()` sets and restores the Spatie team id only when `db_per_tenant` is true, so the shared table's **NULL-team** roles are the rows step 4 classifies |
| Steps | **1–4 only** — rename → create → orphan report → adopt. The `! $perTenant` branch of `plan()` returns before step 5, and `verifyAdmin()` is gated on `$plan->perTenant`, so `roles_created`, `templates_applied`, `templates_removed`, `replacements_granted`, `admin_changes` and `marker_written` are all **0** by construction |
| Lock | `PermissionWriteLock::acquire(COMPAT_SCOPE)` — one global advisory key for one shared table, which is the correct serialization domain here |
| Scale | one run for the whole installation. There is **no** `--compat-fleet` and there must not be one |
| Marker | `PERMISSIONS-SYNC tenant=compat mode=APPLY\|DRY_RUN outcome=…` — the reserved `COMPAT_SCOPE` token, never a UUID, so a log reader can never mistake a global run for a tenant run |
| Cache flush | global `forgetCachedPermissions()` with **no** team set — there is one namespace, and it is the one being flushed |
| Deploy runbook | **deliberately absent from `permission-deploy-runbook.yaml`.** That file is the authority for the **production** deploy sequence, and compatibility mode is **not a production topology** — it is the pre-T6-Phase-0b shape kept alive for legacy installations and local single-schema work. Adding it under `remediation:` would additionally violate the runbook's own rule (a), which requires an explicit `--tenant=`/`--tenants=` argument on any `permissions:sync` row |

**`permissions:sync-fleet` refuses in compatibility mode**, with a typed message naming the command that works — `outcome=BLOCKED reason=compatibility_mode_has_no_fleet`, exit 2 — rather than iterating a directory of tenants whose databases do not exist and reporting every one of them `tenant_not_ready:database_absent`.

### Step 4 — `permissions:sync-fleet`, on 0b's runner, unchanged

**Wave 1 passes it a different closure and a different marker prefix, and nothing more** — the universe algorithm, the selector contract, the readiness predicates, the failure isolation, the population gauge and the exit rule are **already** written and already tested. **`--force` does not exist and is not added.** A sync that is additive, template-delta and idempotent has nothing for a force flag to override, and W-LOT-A-1a's tenth guard rail is literally *"no force option exists"*.

> **The closure takes the hydrated `Tenant`, not a `string`** (plan gate r3, BLOCKER **B3-1**; orchestrator ruling **[R]**). Wave 0b's committed producer declares `Closure(Tenant): FleetTenantOutcome` and invokes it with the model it already resolved and initialized — `@param Closure(Tenant): FleetTenantOutcome $perTenant` at `2f172dd5e:docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-10-rbac-wave-0b.md:1486` and `:1570`, and the call itself is `return $perTenant($tenant);` at `:1593`. Rev 3 supplied `function (string $tenantId)`. Under `declare(strict_types=1)` a `Tenant` object cannot satisfy a `string` parameter, so **every** fleet tenant would have raised a `TypeError`, which 0b's own `catch (Throwable)` at `:1594-1595` converts into `FleetTenantOutcome::failed($tenantId, 'exception:…')` — a silent, total failure: the entrypoint's dry run reaches its `blocked` arm, the apply never runs, Phase 1.10 is not green in isolation and the staging push path is non-functional. **The closure therefore accepts `Tenant $tenant` and derives `(string) $tenant->id` itself**, and that derived id is what reaches both the service and the marker line — the runner's own `runOne()` derives the same value the same way at `:1572`.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services\PermissionSyncService;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Application\DTOs\FleetTenantOutcome;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Application\Services\TenantFleetRunner;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Enums\FleetOutcome;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Tenant;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;

final class SyncPermissionsFleet extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'permissions:sync-fleet
        {--tenants=* : limit to these tenant ids or slugs; omit for every tenant}
        {--dry-run   : compute and report every tenant plan; write nothing}';

    protected $description = 'Apply the permission catalogue and the template delta across the tenant fleet.';

    private const MARKER_PREFIX = 'PERMISSIONS-SYNC-FLEET';

    public function __construct(
        private readonly TenantFleetRunner $runner,
        private readonly PermissionSyncService $sync,
    ) {
        parent::__construct();
    }

    public function handle(): int
    {
        // Compatibility mode has ONE shared roles table and no per-tenant
        // database to iterate, so there is no fleet (plan gate r3, B3-6).
        if (! (bool) config('tenancy_resolver.db_per_tenant')) {
            $this->error('PERMISSIONS-SYNC-FLEET outcome=BLOCKED reason=compatibility_mode_has_no_fleet');
            $this->line('In compatibility mode every tenant shares one roles table and no tenant database exists to '
                .'initialize. Run `php artisan permissions:sync --compat` once, globally, instead.');

            return 2;
        }

        $write = ! (bool) $this->option('dry-run');
        /** @var list<string> $selectors */
        $selectors = $this->option('tenants');

        // 0b's runner declares `Closure(Tenant): FleetTenantOutcome` and calls it
        // with the hydrated, already-initialized model (0b rev 4 :1486,1570,1583).
        // A `string $tenantId` parameter would TypeError on every tenant and be
        // swallowed into `exception:` failures (plan gate r3, B3-1).
        $result = $this->runner->run($selectors, self::MARKER_PREFIX, function (Tenant $tenant) use ($write): FleetTenantOutcome {
            $tenantId = (string) $tenant->id;

            $syncResult = $this->sync->sync($tenantId, $write);
            $marker = $syncResult->markerLine($tenantId, $write);
            $this->line($marker);
            Log::channel('stderr')->info($marker);

            return match ($syncResult->outcome->value) {
                'APPLIED' => FleetTenantOutcome::applied($tenantId, $syncResult->reason),
                'ALREADY_CURRENT' => FleetTenantOutcome::alreadyCurrent($tenantId, $syncResult->reason),
                'BLOCKED' => FleetTenantOutcome::blocked($tenantId, $syncResult->reason),
                default => FleetTenantOutcome::failed($tenantId, $syncResult->reason),
            };
        });

        // The COMMAND renders the aggregate line and owns the exit code — never the runner.
        $aggregate = sprintf(
            '%s mode=%s tenants=%d applied=%d already_current=%d blocked=%d failed=%d provisioned_during_run=%d',
            self::MARKER_PREFIX,
            $write ? 'APPLY' : 'DRY_RUN',
            $result->tenants(),
            $result->count(FleetOutcome::Applied),
            $result->count(FleetOutcome::AlreadyCurrent),
            $result->count(FleetOutcome::Blocked),
            $result->count(FleetOutcome::Failed),
            $result->provisionedDuringRun,
        );
        $this->line($aggregate);
        Log::channel('stderr')->info($aggregate);

        foreach ($result->outcomes as $outcome) {
            if (! $outcome->outcome->isGreen()) {
                $this->error(sprintf('  %s %s reason=%s', $outcome->outcome->value, $outcome->subject, $outcome->reason));
            }
        }

        if ($result->count(FleetOutcome::Failed) > 0) {
            return 1;
        }

        return $result->count(FleetOutcome::Blocked) > 0 ? 2 : 0;
    }
}
~~~

### Step 5 — the deletions, and the ONE test they force to change shape

```
git rm apps/api/app/Console/Commands/EnsurePermissions.php \
       apps/api/app/Console/Commands/EnsurePermissionsFleet.php \
       apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/EnsurePermissionsRunner.php \
       apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/DryRunRollback.php \
       apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/AdminRoleMissing.php \
       apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Application/DTOs/EnsurePermissionsResult.php \
       apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Domain/Enums/EnsurePermissionsOutcome.php \
       scripts/permissions-ensure-0b.sh \
       apps/api/tests/Feature/Identity/EnsurePermissionsCustomisationGuardTest.php \
       apps/api/tests/Feature/Identity/EnsurePermissionsIdempotencyTest.php \
       apps/api/tests/Feature/Tenant/EnsurePermissionsFleetTest.php
git mv apps/api/tests/Feature/Identity/EnsurePermissionsRaceTest.php \
       apps/api/tests/Feature/Identity/PermissionWriteLockConcurrentWriterTest.php
```

> **`PermissionsSyncSupersedesEnsureTest` cannot INVOKE `permissions:ensure`, because this commit deletes it.** It therefore **constructs the state `ensure` leaves** and syncs on top of it — which is a strictly better test, because it asserts against the *end state* the 0b deploy actually produced rather than against a command's behaviour. The fixture builder is:
>
> ```php
> /** The tenant state an ensure-provisioned tenant is in: every catalogue key in its
>  *  PRE-rename spelling, and every seeded template holding exactly version0(). */
> private function seedEnsureProvisionedTenant(string $tenantId): void
> {
>     // 327 permission rows: the 306 lane keys in their PRE-rename spelling
>     // (eleven of them are catalog_cart.* / pos_held_orders.* / *.edit), wave
>     // 0b's nineteen, which `ensure` created, and T2's two transfer keys, which
>     // the merged lane's seeder created.
>     foreach (LegacyRoleBaselineFixture::preRenameCatalogue() as $key) {
>         Permission::create(['name' => $key, 'guard_name' => 'sanctum']);
>     }
>     foreach (SystemRoleTemplate::cases() as $template) {
>         $role = Role::create(['name' => $template->value, 'guard_name' => 'sanctum', $team => $tenantId]);
>         $role->syncPermissions(LegacyRoleBaseline::version0($template->value));
>     }
> }
> ```
>
> | Run | Expected |
> |---|---|
> | first `permissions:sync` on that tenant | `outcome=APPLIED`; **`created=0`** — the mechanical proof that `ensure` left nothing for sync to create; **`renamed=11`** — the full rename map, because `ensure` **never renames** and every source is present with no target present; `adopted_pristine + adopted_customised` = 8, the number of template-named roles; **`adopted_customised=0`**, because the eleven renames are absorbed by `canonical()` on both sides and `ensure`'s equality-guarded grants are **inside** `version0` by construction; `templates_applied` counts only the version-0 → version-1 advance; `templates_removed` counts the templates losing a deprecated key; `marker_written` is 0 or 1 by whether the tenant was marked |
> | second `permissions:sync` | `ALREADY_CURRENT`, every `mutations` counter zero, **zero database writes** by query-log count; the two gauges asserted **unchanged from the first run**, not zero |
>
> `created=0` is the real superseding claim. `renamed=11` is the second half a gate should check: it is the number that proves the two commands have **disjoint jobs** rather than overlapping ones. Expecting `renamed=0` would ship a red test.

### Step 6 — the tests

- [ ] **EC-B2-1a** — `PermissionsSyncTest::a_dry_run_and_an_apply_report_the_same_line`: on the **same** starting tenant state (arranged twice, in two tenant databases, so neither run perturbs the other), `permissions:sync --dry-run` and `permissions:sync` emit **byte-identical** `PERMISSIONS-SYNC` lines apart from the `mode=` token. This is the property the planner exists for, and it is one `assertSame` over two strings with `mode=DRY_RUN`/`mode=APPLY` normalised out.
- [ ] **EC-B2-1b** — `PermissionSyncAdoptionTest::a_dry_run_on_a_legacy_tenant_reports_every_delta_the_apply_performs`: arrange EC-9c's tenant — seven `createLegacyRoles()` roles, every `template_key` NULL, **no `general_manager`** — and run `--dry-run`. Assert `adopted_pristine=6`, `adopted_customised=1`, `roles_created=1`, `marker_written=1`, `templates_applied >= 1`, `outcome=APPLIED`, **exit 0**, and **zero rows written** by row count on `permissions`, `roles` and `role_has_permissions`. **This is the B2-1 red**: against rev 2's service the same arrangement reports `templates_applied=0` and, on a tenant whose `admin` is also missing, `outcome=FAILED reason=admin_role_missing` with exit 1. Paste both the red and the green into the handback.
- [ ] **EC-B2-1c** — `PermissionSyncServiceTest::a_non_green_apply_rolls_the_whole_run_back`: force the post-apply admin verification to fail and assert **nothing at all** committed. The forcing is a real PostgreSQL trigger the test installs in `setUp` and drops in `tearDown` — no test double, no mock, and it reproduces exactly the *"concurrent or inconsistent state"* B2-1 named:
      ```sql
      CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION w1_drop_admin() RETURNS trigger AS $$
      BEGIN
          IF NEW.name = 'admin' THEN
              DELETE FROM roles WHERE id = NEW.id;
          END IF;
          RETURN NULL;
      END;
      $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
      CREATE TRIGGER w1_drop_admin AFTER INSERT ON roles FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION w1_drop_admin();
      ```
      Arrange a tenant with **no** `admin` role, run `permissions:sync` (apply). Step 5 plans and creates it, the trigger deletes it, `verifyAdmin()` finds nothing. Assert: `outcome=FAILED`, `reason=admin_role_missing`, **exit 1**, and `SELECT count(*)` on `permissions`, `roles` and `role_has_permissions` **identical to the pre-run counts** — including the 327 permission rows step 2 would otherwise have created. **Against rev 2's service this test fails with 327 committed `permissions` rows and seven committed adoptions**, because `stepAdmin()` set `FAILED` and returned and the transaction committed. It is the named red for B2-1's second half.
      > It is an `AFTER INSERT` trigger returning `NULL` on purpose, not a `BEFORE INSERT` one: a `BEFORE` trigger that suppresses the insert makes `Role::create()`'s `insert … returning "id"` produce no row, and the run would fail as `reason=exception:…` instead of on the branch under test.
- [ ] `PermissionsSyncTest::a_second_run_writes_no_row` — **zero write statements by query-log count** plus every `mutations` counter zero, and deliberately asserting **nothing** about the two gauges. **This is the B1-3 red at the service level**: with rev 1's unconditional `template_version` update it fails with seven writes.
- [ ] `PermissionsSyncTest::sync_creates_every_registry_permission_in_a_fresh_tenant_and_in_a_second_tenant` — **two physical tenant databases**, asserting data meaning; tenant B's customised `manager` is skipped independently of tenant A's.
- [ ] `PermissionsSyncTest::the_cache_is_flushed_after_commit_not_before` — asserted by observing a concurrent read on a **second connection**.
- [ ] **`PermissionsSyncTest::the_cache_is_flushed_for_this_tenant_under_a_nested_outer_transaction`** — the **B3-5 oracle**, and the case the second-connection test above **cannot** reach, because it never nests. Arrange it exactly as the compatibility registration path does (`dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/AuthController.php:362,488-492`): open an **outer** `DB::transaction()`, set the registrar's permissions team to some **other** tenant `A` inside it, call `sync($tenantB, write: true)` **within** that outer transaction, and let the outer transaction commit. Assert, with a spy or a fake `PermissionRegistrar` that records `(teamId, call)` pairs: (a) `forgetCachedPermissions()` was called **exactly once**, (b) the team id **at the moment of that call** was **`$tenantB`** — not `A`, not `null` — and (c) the team id **after** the callback returned is back to whatever it was when the callback started. **Against rev 3's `registerCacheFlushAfterCommit()` assertion (b) fails with `A`**, because Laravel defers the callback to the root commit (`DatabaseTransactionsManager::afterCommitCallbacksShouldBeExecuted()` returns true only at level 0, `:243-246`) while `sync()`'s own `finally` restored `A` long before. Paste the red into the handback.
- [ ] **`PermissionSyncAdoptionTest::a_legacy_tenant_holding_rename_sources_plans_the_same_delta_it_applies`** — the **B3-2 oracle**, and it uses the tree's real data rather than an invented rename. Arrange a legacy tenant whose `manager` holds **`uom.edit`** and **`deliveries.edit`** (W-LOT's seeder grants exactly those: `lane/w-lot-a-1a:apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php:681,684`) while the wave-1 `manager` template carries **`uom.update`** and **`deliveries.update`** (`:1316,1392`), and both `from` rows exist in `permissions` with neither `to` row present, so step 1 plans the renames. Then assert, in this order: (a) `--dry-run` reports `outcome=APPLIED`, exit 0, `renamed >= 2`, and a `manager` delta that does **not** name either `.edit` key as `removed` or either `.update` key as `added` — the canonicalised projection already equals the template on those two; (b) the **apply** on the same starting state reports a **byte-identical** marker line apart from `mode=`; (c) `outcome` is **not** `FAILED` and `reason` is **not** `plan_divergence:manager`; and (d) the role's final grant set contains the two `.update` keys and neither `.edit` key. **Against rev 3's planner (b) and (c) both fail**: the dry run reports green with `+uom.update −uom.edit`, and the apply rolls back with `reason=plan_divergence:manager`, because the executor renamed the row in place and `TemplateDeltaApplier` re-read a set that already matched. Paste the red into the handback — this is the case that would have hit **every ordinary legacy tenant** on the first fleet apply.
- [ ] `PermissionSyncAdoptionTest::a_renamed_pristine_role_adopts_as_pristine`.
- [ ] `PermissionSyncAdoptionTest::a_role_holding_exactly_the_0b_additions_adopts_as_pristine`.

That is a real, **non-normalisable** difference from v0, so `manager` adopts **customised** and the other six adopt pristine; step 5 then creates `general_manager` and writes the marker (`roles_created=1`, `marker_written=1`).
- [ ] `PermissionSyncMarkerTest` — the **five negatives** (never cleared, never changed, never widened to another role, never widened to another source, never written on a role sync did not create in the same statement) and the **one positive**.
- [ ] `PermissionSyncServiceTest::a_marked_tenant_sees_no_provisioning_source_write` — asserted **by query log**.
- [ ] **The missing-admin oracle is TWO cases, because the one rev 3 named is impossible** (plan gate r3, MAJOR **M3-1**; orchestrator ruling **[R]**). Rev 3 asked `a_missing_admin_role_fails_closed` to install the same `AFTER INSERT` trigger as EC-B2-1c and then expect **exit 1 on the applying AND the dry-run path**. A dry run executes **no insert**, so that trigger can never fire on it; step 5 plans a *virtual* admin, the planning invariant does not trip, and the dry run is **green** by design. The oracle is therefore split, and the *"fails closed on both paths"* half is re-homed onto a **planning-time** failure, which is the only kind a write-free path can have:
      - **`a_missing_admin_role_is_planned_and_previews_green_on_the_dry_run_path`** — a tenant with **no** `admin` role, **no** trigger, `permissions:sync --dry-run`. Assert `roles_created` counts the planned `admin`, `outcome=APPLIED`, **exit 0**, and **zero rows written** on `permissions`, `roles` and `role_has_permissions` by row count. This is what step 5's virtual-state creation *means*, and asserting a failure here would have pinned a behaviour the design deliberately does not have;
      - **`a_missing_admin_role_fails_closed_on_apply`** — the same tenant, the EC-B2-1c `AFTER INSERT` trigger installed, `permissions:sync` (apply). Assert `outcome=FAILED`, `reason=admin_role_missing`, **exit 1**, and the three row counts **identical to the pre-run counts**. This is `verifyAdmin()`'s reachable refusal and it is 0b's `AdminRoleMissing` behaviour preserved through the sentinel's deletion. **The exception class that carries it out of the transaction is `SyncRollback`**, and the zero-write half is proved by row counts, not by "no exception was thrown". It is the same arrangement as EC-B2-1c and shares its `setUp`/`tearDown` trigger helper; keeping both names is deliberate, because EC-B2-1c's subject is *"a non-green apply rolls the whole run back"* and this one's is *"a missing admin is what makes it non-green"*;
      - **the dry run's fail-closed half is proved by EC-7 instead** — a rename collision is a **planning-time** refusal, so `--dry-run` reports `outcome=BLOCKED`, `reason=rename_target_exists:…`, **exit 2** and writes nothing. That, not a trigger, is the evidence that a non-green dry run is reachable and stops a deploy. Say so where EC-7 is listed.

This is the refusal that makes keying the virtual state by role name sound, and it must exist as a test or the assumption is undefended.

Then the mirror case: starting from **central** (`tenancy()->initialized === false`), `--tenant=B` leaves `tenancy()->initialized === false` afterwards — `end()` is correct **there** and only there. **Against rev 3's `finally { if ($initialized && tenancy()->initialized) { tenancy()->end(); } }` clauses (a), (b) and (c) all fail**: Stancl's `initialize(B)` already ended A (`vendor/stancl/tenancy/src/Tenancy.php:48-50`), the `finally` then ended B, and the process is left in **no** tenant context.       - **EC-21c** — the **three refusals**, one assertion each: `permissions:sync --tenant=<x>` → exit 2 `reason=compatibility_mode_is_global`; `permissions:sync` with **no** flag → exit 2 `reason=compat_requires_flag` (**against rev 3 this is exit 1 `no_tenant_context`** — the named red, and the reason an operator had no path at all); `permissions:sync-fleet` → exit 2 `reason=compatibility_mode_has_no_fleet` with **zero** tenant iterations by query log. Plus the mirror refusal **under database-per-tenant**: `permissions:sync --compat` → exit 2 `reason=compat_flag_in_db_per_tenant_mode`;
      - **EC-21d** — **no permissions team is set at any point** during a `--compat` run (`PermissionRegistrar::getPermissionsTeamId()` observed before, during — via a spy on the write path — and after, all `null`), and the after-commit flush is the **global** `forgetCachedPermissions()` with no team. This is what makes the shared table's NULL-team roles visible to step 4 at all.
