Commit message, in full:

```
Phase 1.10: land permissions:sync and permissions:sync-fleet, and delete the whole wave-0b stopgap in the same commit

Wave 0b's binding cutover promise is that the ensure files and their deploy rows
disappear in the commit that introduces their replacement. This commit does that
atomically: PermissionSyncService, both commands and SyncRollback land, and
all EIGHT stopgap artefacts are removed — EnsurePermissions, EnsurePermissionsFleet,
EnsurePermissionsRunner, DryRunRollback, AdminRoleMissing, EnsurePermissionsResult,
EnsurePermissionsOutcome and scripts/permissions-ensure-0b.sh.

The dry-run path is never sentinel-less: SyncRollback replaces DryRunRollback in
the same diff, with the same contract — write-free, not failure-free, so a BLOCKED
dry run still exits 2 and a FAILED one still exits 1.

sync() PLANS before it writes. One plan(), computed over a virtual state, produces
the SyncPlan both paths report; execute() performs it only when the caller asked
for a write and the plan is green. A dry run is therefore the same computation as
an apply, not a differently-shaped one: a legacy tenant is adopted and its missing
roles created IN THE VIRTUAL STATE, so the dry run reports the deltas the apply
would perform instead of skipping them and falsely reporting admin_role_missing.

Every non-green outcome leaves the transaction by throwing SyncRollback, so a
BLOCKED or FAILED apply commits nothing at all — including the permission rows
and adoptions steps 1-4 would otherwise have written before step 8 refused.

Every grant set the planner compares is CANONICAL. Step 1 renames the permission
row IN PLACE at execution time and the pivot follows the primary key, so the
applier re-reads post-rename names; a planner comparing pre-rename names computes
a delta the applier does not, and every legacy tenant whose template holds a
rename source (manager holds uom.edit and deliveries.edit) rolled back with
plan_divergence after a green dry run. canonicalise() is applied to each virtual
role's grants, to the template and admin targets, and to both sides of the
executor's divergence check.

AdminRoleMissing does not vanish, it changes shape, and it gains a reachable home:
verifyAdmin() re-reads the admin role under the lock AFTER execute() and refuses
with admin_role_missing or admin_not_current, which rolls the whole run back.
a_missing_admin_role_fails_closed_on_apply and
::a_non_green_apply_rolls_the_whole_run_back pin both halves by row count; the
dry-run half is a PLANNING failure (EC-7's rename collision), because a dry run
performs no insert and an AFTER INSERT trigger can never fire on it.

permissions:sync --tenant= now resolves the central Tenant by id OR slug and calls
tenancy()->initialize() before the service. In `finally` it RESTORES the tenant it
found rather than ending one: Stancl's initialize() already ends the active tenant
and end() nulls it, so ending in `finally` after initialize(B) destroys tenant A.
It calls end() only when the command started from central context.

Compatibility mode gets an explicit executable path, permissions:sync --compat:
global, central-context, steps 1-4, no tenancy init, no fleet, no readiness
predicate, no permissions team (the shared table's roles are NULL-team), marker
token tenant=compat. It is the non-production topology and is deliberately absent
from the deploy runbook. permissions:sync-fleet refuses in that mode with
reason=compatibility_mode_has_no_fleet rather than reporting every tenant
database_absent.

permissions:sync-fleet's closure takes the hydrated Tenant the runner hands it,
not a string: TenantFleetRunner declares Closure(Tenant): FleetTenantOutcome and
calls it with the model, so a string parameter TypeErrors on every tenant and is
swallowed into exception: outcomes — a fleet that fails totally and silently.

The after-commit cache flush captures the tenant id and sets/restores the Spatie
team INSIDE the callback. Laravel runs after-commit callbacks only at root level
zero, and the compatibility registration path already wraps tenant initialization
in an outer transaction, so relying on the outer scope's team id flushed the wrong
S-1 namespace on exactly the path provisioning uses.

Three tests are deleted because their SUBJECT is a deleted command, each with a
named successor; EnsurePermissionsRaceTest is RENAMED to
PermissionWriteLockConcurrentWriterTest rather than deleted, because its subject is
EC-20b/EC-20c — the lost-edit races on RoleController and UserController — which
survive this wave untouched.

PermissionsSyncSupersedesEnsureTest cannot invoke permissions:ensure (this commit
deletes it), so it CONSTRUCTS the state ensure leaves and syncs on top: created=0
proves ensure left nothing to create, renamed=11 proves the two commands had
disjoint jobs. Expecting renamed=0 would have shipped a red test.

TemplateDeltaApplier's template_version write is conditional (Phase 1.8), so a
second run is ALREADY_CURRENT with zero writes by query-log count — the invariant
an unconditional UPDATE silently broke.

Spec 4.3.1, 4.3.2, 4.3.3, 4.3.5.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01Bp1WuD39YqaMWiHR8vPDNN
```

---

## Task 1.11: the two triggers — provisioning, the entrypoint, and the health check

**Exactly two sync triggers, and a rolling migration is neither.**

1. **Provisioning.** `TenantInitializationService::seedRolesAndPermissionsIfMissing()` (`dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Tenant/Application/Services/TenantInitializationService.php:199-217`, called at `dev:…:75`) is **replaced** by a constructor-injected `PermissionSyncService::sync($tenantId, write: true)` — no `Artisan::call('db:seed')`, no *"if admin exists"* guard, because sync is idempotent and needs no guard. The call **keeps its position at `:75`**, i.e. before `assignDefaultRoles()` at `dev:…:78`, so the role exists when it is assigned.

   **Marker ownership, stated once so the three contracts agree:** `PermissionSyncService::sync()` **returns** a result and **emits no marker** — marker rendering belongs to the *caller*, which is why the marker line lives on the command and why the fleet command, not the service, renders the aggregate line. Provisioning does not go through the command, so **`TenantInitializationService` renders the returned result** into the `PERMISSIONS-SYNC` line (`mode=APPLY`, on the log channel **and** the durable stderr channel), exactly once per provisioning. This adds **no third trigger** — it is trigger 1 reporting the call it already makes.

   **And then it THROWS on any non-green outcome** (plan gate r2, BLOCKER **B2-3**). Rev 2's replacement rendered the result and returned, so initialization walked on to `assignDefaultRoles()` at `dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Tenant/Application/Services/TenantInitializationService.php:78` with a partial catalogue; if the tenant happened to have an `admin` row the assignment succeeded and **provisioning reported success over a half-synced tenant** — contradicting the accepted assertion that a provisioning-time sync failure surfaces as a *failed tenant creation*, and making the proposed `TenantProvisioningSyncTest` case at `:6342-6345` unprovable.

   The named exception is **`TenantPermissionSyncFailed`**, a new `RuntimeException` under `App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Exceptions\`, carrying the tenant id, the outcome and the reason so the caller's log line and the operator's remediation command can both be built from it:

   ~~~php
   <?php

   declare(strict_types=1);

   namespace App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Exceptions;

   use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\PermissionSyncOutcome;
   use RuntimeException;

   /**
    * Provisioning FAILS CLOSED on a non-green permission sync (plan gate r2, B2-3).
    *
    * A tenant whose catalogue is half-applied is worse than a tenant that does
    * not exist: its roles are gated on keys nobody holds, and the operator has
    * no signal that anything went wrong. Throwing here makes the provisioning
    * transaction roll back and the tenant NOT be reported provisioned.
    */
   final class TenantPermissionSyncFailed extends RuntimeException
   {
       public function __construct(
           public readonly string $tenantId,
           public readonly PermissionSyncOutcome $outcome,
           public readonly string $reason,
       ) {
           parent::__construct(sprintf(
               'Permission sync did not complete for tenant %s: outcome=%s reason=%s. '
               .'Re-run `php artisan permissions:sync --tenant=%s` after resolving it.',
               $tenantId,
               $outcome->value,
               $reason,
               $tenantId,
           ));
       }
   }
   ~~~

   ~~~php
   // TenantInitializationService — constructor gains one dependency
   public function __construct(
       // (every existing dependency, unchanged)
       private readonly PermissionSyncService $permissionSync,
   ) {}

   /**
    * Replaces seedRolesAndPermissionsIfMissing(). Sync is idempotent, so the
    * "does admin already exist" guard is gone: a guard that skips the whole
    * catalogue because one role happens to exist is exactly the shape of I-20.
    *
    * LOG FIRST, THEN THROW (plan gate r2, B2-3). The marker is the operator's
    * only record of what the failed run planned, so it is emitted on both
    * channels before the exception unwinds — but it is NOT a substitute for
    * failing: assignDefaultRoles() must never run against a partial catalogue.
    */
   private function syncPermissionsForTenant(Tenant $tenant): void
   {
       $tenantId = (string) $tenant->id;
       $result = $this->permissionSync->sync($tenantId, write: true);
       $marker = $result->markerLine($tenantId, true);

       Log::info($marker);
       Log::channel('stderr')->info($marker);

       if (! $result->outcome->isGreen()) {
           throw new TenantPermissionSyncFailed($tenantId, $result->outcome, $result->reason);
       }
   }
   ~~~
   and at `:75`, `$this->seedRolesAndPermissionsIfMissing();` becomes `$this->syncPermissionsForTenant($tenant);`. The private method, the `Artisan` import and the `RolesAndPermissionsSeeder` import are **deleted, not re-pointed** — that is reference 7 of Task 1.14's census.

   > **Why a throw rather than an early `return false`.** `sync()` already rolled its own transaction back, so nothing of the catalogue landed; what must not happen is the *rest* of provisioning proceeding. The initialization path's existing failure contract is exceptional — `TenancyResolver` throws `TenantUnavailableException` on the same class of problem (`dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Tenant/Application/Services/TenancyResolver.php:94-96`) — so an exception is the shape the callers already handle, and it needs no new branch in any of them.

2. **Deploy.** The entrypoint block under `SYNC_PERMISSIONS_ON_BOOT`, and nothing else.

**There is NO `DatabaseMigrated` listener.** `tenants:migrate` dispatches that event once per tenant on **every** invocation including a no-op one, and `RollingTenantMigrationCommand` calls `tenants:migrate` once per tenant from an entrypoint line that is **unconditional** (`dev:apps/api/docker/entrypoint.sh:150`) and runs **before** the flagged block. With a listener, the flag `false` would still sync every rolling-migrated tenant and `true` would sync every tenant **twice**, and neither listener run would be represented by the status file — OQ-4 would then be a question about a flag that does not govern the behaviour it names.

### The entrypoint block

`dev:apps/api/docker/entrypoint.sh:156-170` — the comment block plus the flagged `tenants:seed` call — is replaced. The surrounding lines are **not**: `:150-154` runs `tenants:migrate-rolling --force` **unconditionally** and catches its failure, which is exactly why a not-ready tenant must be a fleet **failure** rather than a skip.

The `mkdir` goes beside the existing runtime-directory block at `dev:…:19-23`, **not** inside the flagged block, so the directory exists even on a boot that never runs a sync and `HealthCheckService` never reads a path whose parent is missing.

~~~sh
# --- beside the existing `mkdir -p /var/run` at entrypoint.sh:19-23 -------
mkdir -p /var/run/autoerp
chmod 755 /var/run/autoerp
~~~

**THE DRY RUN IS INSIDE THE BLOCK, AND IT GATES THE APPLY** (plan gate r2, MAJOR **M2-5**). The authoritative deploy sequence is migrate → dry-run → apply, and rev 2 satisfied it only for an operator typing three commands. It does not hold for the trigger the soak actually uses: an image push starts the entrypoint, the entrypoint ran the applying `permissions:sync-fleet` directly, and **no operator can get a command into that container before it mutates every tenant**. So the sequencing has to live where the trigger lives.

The mechanism, stated so a reviewer can check it against the shell rather than against this paragraph:

| # | What the block does | Why |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `permissions:sync-fleet --dry-run`, capturing its exit status | this is deploy step 2, executed by the same process that will execute step 3, so nothing can run between them |
| 2 | on a **non-zero** dry run: write `permissions_sync=blocked`, print the marker, **skip the apply**, and keep booting | *"a non-zero dry run means the applying run must not be started"* is already the Deploy table's rule; the container still serves traffic, and the admin health endpoint reports the state |
| 3 | on a **zero** dry run: run the applying `permissions:sync-fleet` and write `ok` or `failed` from **its** status | unchanged from rev 2 |

`tenants:migrate-rolling --force` is deploy step 1 and already runs **unconditionally, above this block**, at `dev:apps/api/docker/entrypoint.sh:150-154` — so the full migrate → dry-run → apply order is a property of the file, and `EntrypointPermissionSyncShapeTest` asserts it by **relative line position**, not by comment.

~~~sh
# --- replacing entrypoint.sh:156-170 -------------------------------------
    # Apply the code-owned permission catalogue and the additive template delta
    # to every tenant database. OPT-IN (SYNC_PERMISSIONS_ON_BOOT=true) while the
    # one-week staging soak runs; OQ-4 decides the production default from the
    # soak's evidence.
    #
    # This REPLACES the destructive `tenants:seed RolesAndPermissionsSeeder`
    # that ran here before (I-20): sync is additive, applies an EXACT diff only
    # to uncustomised template-linked roles, and touches a custom or customised
    # role in exactly one audited case.
    #
    # ORDER IS THE CONTRACT: `tenants:migrate-rolling --force` already ran
    # unconditionally above (entrypoint.sh:150-154), so this block is deploy
    # steps 2 and 3 — dry run, THEN apply, and only if the dry run passed. An
    # image push is the only trigger the soak has, so the gate has to be here;
    # there is no moment at which an operator could interpose a command.
    if [ "$SYNC_PERMISSIONS_ON_BOOT" = "true" ]; then
        echo ""
        echo "Previewing the permission catalogue sync across tenant databases (direct -> $DIRECT_DB_HOST)..."
        if DB_HOST="$DIRECT_DB_HOST" php artisan permissions:sync-fleet --dry-run; then
            echo "  Permission sync preview: [clean - applying]"
            if DB_HOST="$DIRECT_DB_HOST" php artisan permissions:sync-fleet; then
                echo "  Permission sync: [all tenants applied or already current]"
                # SUCCESS OVERWRITES the file. `>` truncates, so a recovered deploy
                # clears a previous boot's failure marker; writing only the failure
                # arm would leave a container that recovered permanently unhealthy.
                printf '%s\n' "permissions_sync=ok" > /var/run/autoerp/permissions-sync.status
            else
                echo "  Permission sync: [FAILED - see the PERMISSIONS-SYNC-FLEET marker above]"
                printf '%s\n' "permissions_sync=failed" > /var/run/autoerp/permissions-sync.status
            fi
        else
            # A non-zero DRY RUN means the applying run must not be started:
            # a rename collision, a not-ready tenant or a missing admin. Nothing
            # was written by the preview and nothing is written now.
            echo "  Permission sync preview: [BLOCKED - apply SKIPPED, see the PERMISSIONS-SYNC-FLEET marker above]"
            printf '%s\n' "permissions_sync=blocked" > /var/run/autoerp/permissions-sync.status
        fi
    fi
~~~

**The entrypoint keeps booting** — refusing to start the container on a permission-sync failure would turn a partial authorization problem into a total outage — **but it stops lying about it**, and it never applies over a preview it could not clear.

### The reader

`HealthCheckService` gains a fifth check in the array at `dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Admin/Application/Services/HealthCheckService.php:19-24`. Because `check()` derives `$allHealthy` from **every** check at `:26`, a failed sync also flips the aggregate — which is the point.

~~~php
    private const SYNC_STATUS_FILE = '/var/run/autoerp/permissions-sync.status';

    /**
     * Read the boot sync's status file. FOUR states, and "absent" is healthy:
     * a container that never ran a boot sync (SYNC_PERMISSIONS_ON_BOOT unset,
     * which is the production default during the soak) is not unhealthy.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private function checkPermissionsSync(): array
    {
        if (! is_file(self::SYNC_STATUS_FILE)) {
            return ['healthy' => true, 'state' => 'not_run'];
        }

        $contents = trim((string) @file_get_contents(self::SYNC_STATUS_FILE));

        if ($contents === 'permissions_sync=failed') {
            return ['healthy' => false, 'error' => 'permission sync reported failure on last boot'];
        }

        // The dry-run gate refused and the apply was SKIPPED (plan gate r2, M2-5).
        // Nothing was written, which is the correct outcome — and it is still
        // unhealthy, because the deploy did not do what the deploy claims to do.
        if ($contents === 'permissions_sync=blocked') {
            return ['healthy' => false, 'error' => 'permission sync preview was blocked on last boot; the apply was skipped'];
        }

        return ['healthy' => $contents === 'permissions_sync=ok', 'state' => $contents];
    }
~~~
and in `check()`'s array: `'permissions_sync' => $this->checkPermissionsSync(),`.

**The key is exposed on the AUTHENTICATED `/api/v1/admin/monitoring/health`** (`apps/api/routes/api.php:83`, inside `['auth:sanctum-admin', 'super_admin', 'throttle:admin-sensitive']`). **The public `/api/v1/health` is unchanged** and stays a two-field liveness probe for the load balancer: a public endpoint must not disclose the platform's internal authorization state, and flipping it to 503 on a permission-sync failure would take the whole fleet out of the load balancer over a *partial* authorization problem — the opposite of "boot continues".

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — the tests first**, then the three edits.
- [ ] **Step 2 — `TenantProvisioningSyncTest`** — a freshly provisioned tenant holds `registry->activeKeys()` on `admin`, **with no boot flag set and no migration event involved**.
- [ ] **Step 3 — EC-40** — a completed provisioning emits **exactly one** `PERMISSIONS-SYNC tenant=<uuid> mode=APPLY outcome=APPLIED` line, and a provisioning that never reached its sync emits **none**.
- [ ] **Step 3a — EC-B2-3** — `TenantProvisioningSyncTest::a_failed_sync_fails_the_provisioning`, the **B2-3 oracle**. Force a non-green sync with the same `w1_drop_admin` PostgreSQL trigger EC-B2-1c installs, then provision a tenant. Assert, in this order:
      1. `TenantPermissionSyncFailed` is thrown, and its message names the tenant id and `reason=admin_role_missing`;
      2. the `PERMISSIONS-SYNC … outcome=FAILED` marker **was** emitted on both channels before the throw (the operator's only record of what the run planned);
      3. **`assignDefaultRoles()` never ran** — no `model_has_roles` row exists for the new tenant's first user;
      4. **the tenant is not reported provisioned** — whatever `TenantInitializationService`'s callers persist as the provisioned state is absent, asserted on the row rather than on a return value.
      **Against rev 2's replacement this test fails at assertion 1** (no throw) **and at 3** (`assignDefaultRoles()` succeeded against a partial catalogue). Paste the red into the handback.
- [ ] **Step 4 — `PermissionSyncTriggerCensusTest`** — a static-analysis assertion that **no** production listener, subscriber or service-provider binding references `Stancl\Tenancy\Events\DatabaseMigrated`, and that the only production callers of `PermissionSyncService::sync()` are `TenantInitializationService`, `SyncPermissions`, `SyncPermissionsFleet` and (from Task 1.14) `RolesAndPermissionsSeeder`.
- [ ] **Step 5 — `EntrypointPermissionSyncShapeTest`** — a shell-source-shape test over `docker/entrypoint.sh`, eight assertions, all by line position or literal match so none of them depends on a comment:
      - the status file is written on **all three** arms (`ok`, `failed`, `blocked`), with `>` (never `>>`);
      - `mkdir -p /var/run/autoerp` appears **outside** the flagged block;
      - the flagged block calls `permissions:sync-fleet` and **never** `tenants:seed`;
      - no `exit` follows any arm;
      - **the `--dry-run` invocation's line number is LESS than the applying invocation's** (M2-5's order, asserted mechanically);
      - **the applying invocation is nested inside the dry run's success branch** — the test asserts the applying line sits between the `if … --dry-run; then` line and its matching `else`;
      - **`tenants:migrate-rolling` appears at a LOWER line number than the `--dry-run` invocation**, which is the migrate → dry-run → apply order as a property of the file;
      - the `blocked` arm contains **no** `permissions:sync-fleet` invocation without `--dry-run`.

## Task 1.12: the `customised_at` writer and *Re-apply template*

**Why the writer cannot wait for wave 2.** Wave 1 already deploys and repeatedly runs sync, so during the soak the existing editor would keep calling `syncPermissions()` **without marking customisation**, and the next sync could add template grants to a role the tenant had just edited. The column and the writer that sets it **must not ship a wave apart**.

**The writer's transaction is the SAME transaction as its `syncPermissions()`, and it takes the shared lock.** Today `RoleController::update` resolves the role at `lane/w-lot-a-1a:…/RoleController.php:239` and later calls `syncPermissions()` at `:265` with **no transaction and no lock at all** — 0b-14 wraps it; wave 1 adds the flag write inside that same wrapper. The operator edit becomes, in one transaction: `PermissionWriteLock::acquire($tenantId)` → `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` the role (ordered `roles.name`, `roles.id`) → compute the audit diff → `syncPermissions()` → `customised_at = now()` → commit. The flag and the grant set it describes are then written **or rolled back together**, which is what lets step 6's post-lock re-read be trusted: there is no window in which the grants have changed and the flag has not.

**Set `customised_at = now()` only when a system role's permission set ACTUALLY CHANGES and `customised_at IS NULL`.** A no-op save must not mark a role customised — that would withhold every future delta from a tenant that changed nothing.

~~~php
// RoleController::update — the permissions branch, replacing lane :263-266
if (isset($validated['permissions'])) {
    /** @var list<string> $requested */
    $requested = array_values($validated['permissions']);
    $tenantId = (string) $this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId();

    DB::transaction(function () use ($role, $requested, $tenantId): void {
        // (i) the lock is the FIRST statement — wave 0b's helper, unchanged.
        $this->writeLock->acquire($tenantId);

        // (ii) re-read the role UNDER the lock, in the lane's row order.
        /** @var Role $locked */
        $locked = Role::query()
            ->whereKey($role->getKey())
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->firstOrFail();

        /** @var list<string> $before */
        $before = $locked->permissions()->toBase()->pluck('name')->all();
        $added = array_values(array_diff($requested, $before));
        $removed = array_values(array_diff($before, $requested));

        // (iii) the grant write.
        $locked->syncPermissions($requested);

        // (iv) the flag, in the SAME transaction. Only on a REAL change, only
        // on a system role, and only once — it records the FIRST edit.
        if (($added !== [] || $removed !== [])
            && (bool) $locked->getAttribute('is_system')
            && $locked->getAttribute('customised_at') === null) {
            DB::table('roles')->where('id', $locked->getKey())->update(['customised_at' => $this->dates->now()]);
        }
    });
}
~~~

***Re-apply template* is a `RoleController` action over one role, not a sync mode.**

~~~php
/**
 * POST /api/v1/roles/{id}/reapply-template
 *
 * Restores one system role to its template. The five obligations, in order and
 * in ONE transaction: refuse unless template-linked; clear customised_at INSIDE
 * the lock so a concurrent sync cannot observe the half-state; apply the exact
 * diff through the ONE applier; advance the version; emit RoleSyncedV1 with a
 * NON-NULL actor, which is the only difference from a sync-emitted event.
 *
 * TWO REV-3 CORRECTIONS, both from plan gate r2 MAJOR M2-2:
 *
 *  (a) the customised_at clear is CONDITIONAL. Rev 2 issued an unconditional
 *      `UPDATE roles SET customised_at = NULL`, so a re-apply on an ALREADY
 *      pristine role at the current version wrote one row — which is exactly
 *      what TemplateReapplyTest's zero-write case forbids. It is now guarded on
 *      `customised_at IS NOT NULL`, and whether it fired is carried out as
 *      $flagCleared;
 *
 *  (b) the audit event covers EVERY EFFECTIVE MUTATION, not only grant changes.
 *      Rev 2 emitted only when $delta->added or $delta->removed was non-empty,
 *      so clearing a non-null customised_at, or advancing template_version
 *      alone, mutated the role with no RoleSyncedV1 row — an invisible state
 *      change in a wave whose whole point is that role mutations are auditable.
 *      The condition is now `$flagCleared || $delta->wrote()`, and TemplateDelta
 *      ::wrote() (Task 1.8) is exactly "this apply wrote ANY row".
 *
 * The already-pristine-and-current path therefore performs NO update and emits
 * NO event, which is what the zero-write assertion measures.
 */
#[CrossTenantRoute(reason: 'Spatie TeamScope auto-scoping: Role::findOrFail($id) is filtered by team_id; a role from another team 404s here.')]
public function reapplyTemplate(Request $request, int $id): JsonResponse
{
    $role = Role::findOrFail($id);

    if (! (bool) $role->getAttribute('is_system') || ! is_string($role->getAttribute('template_key'))) {
        return response()->json([
            'error' => ['code' => 'ROLE_HAS_NO_TEMPLATE', 'message' => 'Only a system role linked to a template can be re-applied.'],
        ], 422);
    }

    $tenantId = (string) $this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId();
    $actorId = $request->user()?->getAuthIdentifier();
    $version = $this->registry->version();

    $delta = DB::transaction(function () use ($role, $tenantId, $version, $actorId): TemplateDelta {
        $this->writeLock->acquire($tenantId);

        /** @var Role $locked */
        $locked = Role::query()->whereKey($role->getKey())->orderBy('name')->orderBy('id')->lockForUpdate()->firstOrFail();

        // (b) clear the flag INSIDE the lock, before the apply — but ONLY if it
        // is set. An unconditional UPDATE writes a row on a re-apply that
        // changes nothing (plan gate r2, M2-2).
        $flagCleared = false;
        if ($locked->getAttribute('customised_at') !== null) {
            DB::table('roles')->where('id', $locked->getKey())->update(['customised_at' => null]);
            $locked->refresh();
            $flagCleared = true;
        }

        $delta = $this->applier->applyTo($locked, $version);

        // EVERY effective mutation is audited, not only a grant change:
        // wrote() is true for added, removed OR a version-only advance, and
        // $flagCleared covers the customisation flag itself (M2-2).
        if ($flagCleared || $delta->wrote()) {
            $this->events->dispatch(new RoleSyncedV1(
                roleId: (int) $locked->getKey(),
                roleName: (string) $locked->getAttribute('name'),
                templateKey: (string) $locked->getAttribute('template_key'),
                added: $delta->added,
                removed: $delta->removed,
                cause: $flagCleared ? 'template_reapply' : 'template_reapply_version_only',
                fromTemplateVersion: $delta->fromVersion,
                toTemplateVersion: $delta->toVersion,
                tenantId: $tenantId,
                actorUserId: $actorId === null ? null : (string) $actorId,
                occurredAtIso: $this->dates->now()->toIso8601String(),
            ));
        }

        return $delta;
    });

    $role->refresh()->load('permissions');

    return response()->json([
        'data' => [
            'role' => (array) $this->roleData($role),
            'added' => $delta->added,
            'removed' => $delta->removed,
            'from_template_version' => $delta->fromVersion,
            'to_template_version' => $delta->toVersion,
        ],
        'meta' => [
            'timestamp' => now()->toIso8601String(),
            'request_id' => $request->header('X-Request-ID', (string) uuid_create()),
        ],
    ]);
}
~~~

and the route, beside the five existing role routes in `apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/routes.php` (the group at `lane/w-lot-a-1a:…:57-64`, which already carries `['api', 'auth:sanctum', SetPermissionsTeam::class, EnforceTokenTenantClaim::class]`):
```php
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\IdentityPermission;

    Route::post('roles/{id}/reapply-template', [RoleController::class, 'reapplyTemplate'])
        ->middleware('can:'.IdentityPermission::RolesManage->value)
        ->name('roles.reapply-template');
```
> **The gate is the ENUM EXPRESSION, not the literal** (plan gate r2, MAJOR **M2-3**). Rev 2 wrote `->middleware('can:roles.manage')` — a dotted literal at a middleware call site, which is precisely what Task 1.16's `ForbidPermissionStringLiteral` makes an error, and Task 1.16's own rule is that the baseline may absorb only **pre-existing** sites and never one this wave introduces. Shipping both would have made the wave's last commit red or its baseline dishonest. `'can:'.IdentityPermission::RolesManage->value` is the form `RoutePermissionLiteralTest` **accepts** by design (its `ConstExprEvaluator` resolves the concat), so this route is also one of that test's named accepted-shape cases.
>
> `roles.manage` is an **existing** catalogue key held by `admin` only, so this route adds no key, does not move either route-coverage ceiling in the *uncovered* direction, and needs no baseline edit — it is a **new** route and it ships **gated**, which is what the ratchet's anti-growth direction requires.

`RoleController`'s constructor gains **five** dependencies — `PermissionWriteLock $writeLock`, `TemplateDeltaApplier $applier`, `PermissionRegistry $registry`, `Dispatcher $events`, `DateFactory $dates` — all `private readonly`, none through `app()`. (Rev 2 said "four" and listed five; plan gate r2, minor.)

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — the tests first**, then the controller changes and the route.
- [ ] **Step 2 — `PermissionWriteLockRaceTest` (EC-20a)**, two PG connections: operator edit versus sync. **The outcome contract, which is what a gate checks rather than the mechanism:** whichever order the two transactions serialize in, if the operator commits first, sync's post-lock re-read sees `customised_at IS NOT NULL` and **skips** the role into the `templates_skipped_customised` gauge; if sync commits first, it applies its delta to a then-pristine role and the operator's edit lands afterwards, setting `customised_at` on top. **There is no interleaving in which a write is lost, and none in which a role is left `customised_at IS NOT NULL` carrying a grant set the operator did not choose.**
- [ ] **Step 3 — `CustomisedAtWriterTest`**: a no-op save does **not** set the flag; a real change does; a second change does **not** move it (it records the **first** edit); a **custom** role is unaffected (it has no template to be customised against); and the flag and the grants are written in **one** transaction (asserted by rolling back and observing neither).
- [ ] **Step 4 — `TemplateReapplyTest`**, six named cases (plan gate r2, **M2-2**):
      - `a_custom_role_is_refused` and `a_system_role_without_a_template_key_is_refused` — 422, `ROLE_HAS_NO_TEMPLATE`;
      - `a_customised_role_is_restored_and_audited` — the flag is cleared, the exact diff applied, the version advanced, and **one** `RoleSyncedV1` row exists with a **non-null** `actor_user_id` and `cause=template_reapply`;
      - **`an_already_pristine_role_at_the_current_version_writes_nothing`** — the M2-2 red. Arrange a role with `customised_at IS NULL`, `template_version = registry->version()` and a grant set already equal to its template. Assert: empty `added`/`removed` in the response, **zero `insert`/`update`/`delete` statements over the whole request by query-log count**, and **zero** new `RoleSyncedV1` audit rows. **Against rev 2's controller this fails with one `UPDATE roles SET customised_at = NULL`**;
      - **`a_version_only_advance_is_audited`** — the second M2-2 half. Arrange a **pristine** role whose grant set already equals its template but whose `template_version` is behind. Assert the version advances, `added`/`removed` are both empty, and **one** `RoleSyncedV1` row exists with `cause=template_reapply_version_only`. **Against rev 2's controller no event is emitted and the row mutation is invisible in the audit chain**;
      - `a_caller_without_roles_manage_gets_403`.
- [ ] **Step 5 — extend `PermissionWriteLockCoverageTest`** using 0b rev 2's three-way split:
      - `LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS` gains `app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/PermissionSyncService.php::sync`, `app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/RoleController.php::update` and `…::reapplyTemplate`;
      - **`LOCK_INHERITED_FROM` gains one row in 0b rev 3's LIST-VALUED shape** — the literal, verbatim:
        ```php
        'app/Modules/Identity/Domain/Authorization/TemplateDeltaApplier.php::applyTo' => [
            'app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/PermissionSyncService.php::sync',
            'app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/RoleController.php::reapplyTemplate',
        ],
        ```
        **Both** named entry points are also in `LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS`, and `PermissionWriterCensus::callersOf($units, 'TemplateDeltaApplier::applyTo')` must return **exactly** those two, tree-wide. 0b rev 3's ruling is **branch 2** — the applier acquires nothing and the completeness of the caller list is what proves the inheritance — so wave 1 must **not** add an `acquire()` to the applier. If the census returns a third caller, the fixture grows and the test names it; falling back to branch 1 is a decision to record, not to make silently;
      - the failure message quotes `PermissionWriterCensus::methodSource($unit)`, not the whole file — that is what 0b rev 3's AST-bounded extraction is for, and wave 1 **consumes** it rather than re-implementing it;
      - **`INITIALIZATION_ONLY` must not grow.**

## Task 1.13: separation of duties

> **SoD-1.** For every `sod_group` in the registry, no **non-`admin`** system-role template may hold both a `create`-class key and a **financially decisive** key (`post`, `reverse`, `void`, `approve`, `confirm`, `refund`, `allocate`, `pay`) from that group. A `legacy()` key participates through its declared `behavesAs`.

**Three scoping decisions, each stated so a gate can challenge them.** (1) **Templates, not roles** — the rule constrains what the platform *ships*; a tenant may still create a custom role holding both, because a five-person pharmacy legitimately has one person who raises and approves POs, and none of Odoo, ERPNext or Dolibarr implements NIST SSD/DSD generically. (2) **`admin` is exempt**, because `admin` holds everything by definition. (3) **Exactly eight groups, and the list is closed for wave 1**: `purchase-order`, `supplier-invoice`, `invoice`, `credit-note`, `payment`, `expense`, `journal`, `stock-adjustment`.

**The group memberships, mechanically, as Task 1.4's manifests declare them** — twenty keys, and `purchase-orders.approve` is **not** among them because it does not exist:

| `sod_group` | Member keys |
|---|---|
| `purchase-order` | `purchase-orders.create`, `purchase-orders.confirm` |
| `supplier-invoice` | `supplier-invoices.create-pending` *(legacy, `behavesAs: Create`)*, `supplier-invoices.approve-invoice-first` *(legacy, `behavesAs: Approve`)* |
| `invoice` | `invoices.create`, `invoices.post` |
| `credit-note` | `credit-notes.create`, `credit-notes.post` |
| `payment` | `payments.create`, `payments.pay-supplier` *(legacy, `behavesAs: Pay`)*, `payments.allocate`, `payments.reverse`, `payments.refund` |
| `expense` | `expenses.create`, `expenses.post`, `expenses.pay` |
| `journal` | `journal.create`, `journal.post` |
| `stock-adjustment` | `inventory.adjustments.create`, `inventory.adjustments.post` |

`payments.reverse` is a member even though **no template holds it** — it is admin-only by an earlier owner decision — so it adds no baseline row while making a future grant of it to `manager` an immediate failure. That is the hard half doing its job before the fact.

**`order`, `delivery`, `income` and `work-order` are deliberately NOT SoD groups**, even though `manager` holds create-plus-decisive combinations in all four. They are **operational** transitions, not financially decisive ones: a sales-order confirmation, a delivery confirmation, an income posting against an already-recorded receipt and a work-order approval each commit the business to *doing work*, not to *money changing hands or a fiscal fact becoming real*. All three reference systems draw the same line. **The cost is stated honestly:** a manager who can raise and confirm a sales order can commit stock without a second pair of eyes. That is an accepted residual — the compensating control is the fiscal chain and the stock-movement audit — and re-opening it means **adding a group**, which the hard half makes a deliberate, reviewed act rather than a drift.

**The test ships in two halves.** **Hard half:** any `sod_group` introduced from wave 1 onward, and any template grant **added** to an existing group, fails immediately. **No baseline entry may be added, ever.** **Ratchet half:** the existing combinations are baselined **by name** in `permission-sod-baseline.json`, counted against a shrink-only `SOD_BASELINE_CEILING = 18`.

**The baseline is EIGHTEEN, re-derived from the manifests of Task 1.4** (eleven on the pre-lane tree; `general_manager`'s grant set is `manager`'s plus two keys, so it inherits the seven `manager` rows verbatim — **none of them a new decision**):

| # | `sod_group` | Template | Create-class key | Decisive key(s) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `journal` | `accountant` | `journal.create` | `journal.post` |
| 2 | `credit-note` | `manager` | `credit-notes.create` | `credit-notes.post` |
| 3 | `credit-note` | `general_manager` | `credit-notes.create` | `credit-notes.post` |
| 4 | `invoice` | `manager` | `invoices.create` | `invoices.post` |
| 5 | `invoice` | `general_manager` | `invoices.create` | `invoices.post` |
| 6 | `expense` | `manager` | `expenses.create` | `expenses.pay`, `expenses.post` |
| 7 | `expense` | `general_manager` | `expenses.create` | `expenses.pay`, `expenses.post` |
| 8 | `expense` | `accountant` | `expenses.create` | `expenses.pay`, `expenses.post` |
| 9 | `stock-adjustment` | `manager` | `inventory.adjustments.create` | `inventory.adjustments.post` |
| 10 | `stock-adjustment` | `general_manager` | `inventory.adjustments.create` | `inventory.adjustments.post` |
| 11 | `purchase-order` | `manager` | `purchase-orders.create` | `purchase-orders.confirm` |
| 12 | `purchase-order` | `general_manager` | `purchase-orders.create` | `purchase-orders.confirm` |
| 13 | `supplier-invoice` | `manager` | `supplier-invoices.create-pending` | `supplier-invoices.approve-invoice-first` |
| 14 | `supplier-invoice` | `general_manager` | `supplier-invoices.create-pending` | `supplier-invoices.approve-invoice-first` |
| 15 | `supplier-invoice` | `accountant` | `supplier-invoices.create-pending` | `supplier-invoices.approve-invoice-first` |
| 16 | `payment` | `manager` | `payments.create` | `payments.allocate`, `payments.pay-supplier`, `payments.refund` |
| 17 | `payment` | `general_manager` | `payments.create` | `payments.allocate`, `payments.pay-supplier`, `payments.refund` |
| 18 | `payment` | `accountant` | `payments.create` | `payments.allocate`, `payments.pay-supplier`, `payments.refund` |

**Four of the eight shipped templates satisfy the rule with no change** — `cashier` holds `invoices.create`, `expenses.create` and `payments.create` but no decisive key in any group; `operator` is likewise create-and-update only; `viewer` and `technician` hold no create-class key in any of the eight groups. **That is the evidence it is a real rule rather than a rule nothing can pass**, and it is verified against the post-0b grant map rather than asserted.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — check O-5/OQ-3 first.** If the owner has ruled, apply the retirement to the manifests' `templateDefaults` (Task 1.4c's `Document` manifest for the invoice/credit-note rows, Task 1.4g's `Treasury` manifest for the payment row) **before** generating the baseline, and pin the ceiling at 15 or 12. If not, ship eighteen. **Do not decide it here.**
- [ ] **Step 2 — generate `permission-sod-baseline.json` from the registry** and assert the generated set **equals** the table above (or the ruled-down table). The ceiling is the count, and it only falls.
      ```
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w1/apps/api && php artisan tinker --execute='
        $r = app(\App\Shared\Domain\Authorization\PermissionRegistry::class);
        $grants = $r->templateGrants(); unset($grants["admin"]);
        $byKey = []; foreach ($r->definitions() as $d) { $byKey[$d->key] = $d; }
        $rows = [];
        foreach ($r->sodGroups() as $group => $keys) {
          foreach ($grants as $template => $held) {
            $c = array_values(array_filter($keys, fn($k) => in_array($k,$held,true) && $byKey[$k]->isCreateClass()));
            $d = array_values(array_filter($keys, fn($k) => in_array($k,$held,true) && $byKey[$k]->isFinanciallyDecisive()));
            if ($c && $d) { sort($c); sort($d); $rows[] = ["sod_group"=>$group,"template"=>$template,"create"=>$c,"decisive"=>$d]; }
          }
        }
        usort($rows, fn($a,$b) => [$a["sod_group"],$a["template"]] <=> [$b["sod_group"],$b["template"]]);
        file_put_contents("tests/Architecture/baselines/permission-sod-baseline.json", json_encode($rows, JSON_PRETTY_PRINT|JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES|JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE)."\n");
        echo count($rows), PHP_EOL;'

Three cases: `no_unbaselined_combination_exists` (the hard half), `the_baseline_is_within_the_shrink_only_ceiling`, and `four_templates_are_clean` (naming `cashier`, `operator`, `viewer`, `technician` — the evidence case).

      php artisan test tests/Feature/Authorization/PermissionSodTemplateTest.php
      ./vendor/bin/pint --test tests/Feature/Authorization/PermissionSodTemplateTest.php

## Task 1.14: `RolesAndPermissionsSeeder` becomes a thin adapter — and the array is deleted

**The class name, namespace and public static signatures are PRESERVED.** W-LOT-A-1a never states a preservation rule for the name; **this design states it**, so a later lane cannot rename the class and quietly break the staging protocol.

**The full dependency census — SEVEN PHP references, not five**, each disposed of by name:

| Reference | Disposition |
|---|---|
| `apps/api/app/Console/Commands/ExportFrontendPermissionsMap.php:29-30` | **re-pointed** at the registry (Task 1.17) |
| `apps/api/database/seeders/DatabaseSeeder.php:76` | verified: `$this->call(RolesAndPermissionsSeeder::class)` still works through the shim |
| `apps/api/database/seeders/DemoTenantSeeder.php:111` | verified |
| `apps/api/database/seeders/CoffeeShopSeeder.php:116` | verified |
| `apps/api/database/seeders/ParapharmacySeeder.php:316` | verified |
| `apps/api/database/seeders/ProductionSeeder.php:70` | verified |
| `apps/api/app/Modules/Tenant/Application/Services/TenantInitializationService.php:213-216` | **DELETED, not re-pointed** — the provisioning trigger became a direct `PermissionSyncService::sync()` call in Task 1.11 |

Plus three non-PHP: the entrypoint `--class` string (`dev:apps/api/docker/entrypoint.sh:165` — **removed with the block** in Task 1.11), two staging `docker exec` heredocs in the lane's evidence protocol, and the lane's mandatory PG preservation test named after the class.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Database\Seeders;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services\PermissionSyncService;
use App\Shared\Domain\Authorization\PermissionRegistry;
use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

/**
 * THIN ADAPTER. The 871-line array is gone; PermissionRegistry owns the
 * catalogue and PermissionSyncService owns the write.
 *
 * The class NAME, NAMESPACE and the two public static SIGNATURES are preserved
 * deliberately: the exporter, five seeders and lane/w-lot-a-1a's five-push
 * staging protocol all name them. W-LOT-A-1a never stated that preservation
 * rule; this design does.
 */
final class RolesAndPermissionsSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly PermissionSyncService $sync,
        private readonly PermissionRegistrar $permissionRegistrar,
    ) {}

    /**
     * Q-w1-3: the lane's MARKED-TENANT PRESERVATION BRANCH is kept VERBATIM.
     * On a marked tenant with LOT_ACTION_PERMISSIONS_ENFORCE off, this emits the
     * WLOTA1A-RESEED marker and RETURNS WITHOUT DELEGATING, so that lane's
     * mandatory PG test (RolesAndPermissionsSeederMarkedTenantTest, which asserts
     * the permission snapshot is BYTE-IDENTICAL after a flag-off reseed) keeps
     * passing. Every other path delegates.
     *
     * The lane's ENFORCEMENT branch, which invoked LotActionPermissionDelta, is
     * DELETED: nothing in wave 1 calls the delta, and
     * PermissionSyncServiceTest::sync_references_no_lot_action_symbol asserts it.
     */
    public function run(): void
    {
        $tenantId = $this->currentTenantId();
        if ($tenantId === null) {
            return;
        }

        if (! (bool) config('lot_action_permissions.enforce')
            && $this->markedTenantCarriesWlota1aDelta($tenantId)) {
            $marker = sprintf(
                'WLOTA1A-RESEED tenant=%s mode=preserve outcome=SKIPPED reason=marked_tenant_delta_preserved',
                $tenantId,
            );
            $this->command?->info($marker);
            Log::channel('stderr')->info($marker);

            return;
        }

        $result = $this->sync->sync($tenantId, write: true);
        $marker = $result->markerLine($tenantId, true);
        $this->command?->info($marker);
        Log::channel('stderr')->info($marker);
    }

    /**
     * @internal Compatibility shim — call PermissionRegistry directly in new code.
     *           `app()` is the ONE sanctioned exception to rule 13 in this wave:
     *           the method is static, so there is no constructor to inject into,
     *           and it exists solely to keep the exporter's and the lane's
     *           contracts intact. Wave 3 migrates the exporter and deletes both.
     *
     * @return list<string>
     */
    public static function permissionNames(): array
    {
        return app(PermissionRegistry::class)->keys();
    }

    /**
     * @internal Compatibility shim — see permissionNames().
     *
     * @return array<string, list<string>>
     */
    public static function rolePermissionGrants(): array
    {
        return app(PermissionRegistry::class)->templateGrants();
    }

    private function currentTenantId(): ?string
    {
        $teamId = $this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId();

        return is_string($teamId) && $teamId !== '' ? $teamId : null;
    }

    /** The lane's predicate, kept here verbatim for the preservation branch above. */
    private function markedTenantCarriesWlota1aDelta(string $tenantId): bool
    {
        return app(\App\Modules\Identity\Infrastructure\Repositories\RoleMarkerRepository::class)
            ->tenantCarriesMarker('w-lot-a-1a');
    }
}
~~~

> **Three `app()` calls, and all three are inside the sanctioned exception**: two `public static` shims with no constructor, and `markedTenantCarriesWlota1aDelta()`, which the preservation branch calls from `run()` and which **could** be constructor-injected. **Inject it.** Add `private readonly RoleMarkerRepository $roleMarkers` to the constructor and call `$this->roleMarkers->tenantCarriesMarker('w-lot-a-1a')`; the `app()` form above is shown only to make the seam visible. The PHPStan `app()`-ban allow-list gains **exactly two** entries: `RolesAndPermissionsSeeder::permissionNames` and `::rolePermissionGrants`.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — delete the 871-line array in the SAME commit that the manifests become authoritative,** and run `PermissionCatalogueParityTest` immediately. It exists exactly to make this cut-over reviewable.
- [ ] **Step 2 — re-point `LegacyRoleBaselineParityTest` at its committed fixture IN THIS COMMIT.** Before it, the assertion is method-versus-class; after it, fixture-versus-class; **it is never a hand copy of an 800-line array.** Leaving it pointed at the shim would turn it into fixture-versus-registry and stop it proving what it was written to prove (this is wave 0b's residual **R-0b-6**).
- [ ] **Step 3 — delete `permissions:dump-baseline`** (`apps/api/app/Console/Commands/DumpLegacyRoleBaseline.php`) — the fixture it produced is now frozen and the lane method it read is a shim.
- [ ] **Step 4 — verify each of the seven references**, by running the seeder or command that reaches it, and record the seven verdicts in the handback. **"It compiles" is not a verdict.** The seven commands:

## Task 1.15: `permissions:scaffold` — the deterministic generator behind contract AI-1

**The honest contract, restated.** Rev 1 of the spec claimed that adding a `PermissionDefinition` *"and nothing else"* produced enum reachability and en/fr/ar labels. **It cannot:** the per-module enums are separately authored classes, the locale files are separate human-edited artifacts, and a brand-new module also needs its manifest tagged in its service provider. A test can **refuse** those omissions; it cannot synthesise them. The contract is therefore **one definition plus one generator run**.

**Five artifacts, not four** (rev 1 said "four" and then listed five). They are: the **manifest entry**, the **enum case**, and **three** locale stubs — `en`, `fr`, `ar`. `permissions:scaffold` writes all five deterministically, and it **refuses** rather than guessing when the module has no manifest or no enum yet, printing the two files to create and the tag line to add.

### The determinism contract, stated as five mechanical rules

| # | Rule | Why it is not optional |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | **The manifest entry is inserted at a FIXED ANCHOR** — the line matching `/^\s*return \[$/` inside `definitions()` — and the resulting list is **re-sorted by `(resource, action)`**, then re-emitted. The command never appends at the cursor and never appends at the end. | A generator whose output depends on where the previous one stopped is not a generator. Sorting means two invocations in either order produce the same file. |
| 2 | **The enum case is appended immediately before the class's closing brace**, i.e. before the LAST `/^\}$/` line of the file, then the case block is re-sorted by case name. | Same reason; and appending before the closing brace is the only insertion point that is well-defined for an enum with no methods. |
| 3 | **JSON is encoded with `JSON_PRETTY_PRINT \| JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES \| JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE`**, keys sorted with `ksort($block, SORT_STRING)`, and the file ends with exactly one `\n`. | `JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE` is required or the Arabic bundle is rewritten into `\uXXXX` escapes on every run and the diff is unreadable; `SORT_STRING` because PHP's default sort is locale-sensitive. |
| 4 | **Every write is atomic**: render to `<path>.scaffold.tmp` in the same directory, `fflush`, then `rename()`. A partial write leaves the original untouched. | A generator interrupted halfway must not corrupt a 300-key locale bundle. |
| 5 | **The command is IDEMPOTENT**: a second invocation with the same arguments detects the key already present in the manifest and exits `0` having written **nothing** (byte-identical mtime-independent check via a content hash before and after). | This is what `ScaffoldPermissionCommandTest`'s two-clean-worktree case actually measures. |

**A brand-new module's provider tag is NOT generated.** The command detects a missing manifest class and **refuses** with the exact three things to do — create `app/Modules/<M>/Domain/Authorization/<M>PermissionManifest.php`, create `app/Modules/<M>/Domain/Enums/<M>Permission.php`, add `$this->app->tag([...], 'permission.manifests');` to `<M>`'s provider — because writing a new class into an unknown provider layout is exactly the kind of guess that produces a file nobody reviews.

**Files:** `apps/api/app/Console/Commands/ScaffoldPermission.php`, `apps/api/tests/Feature/Authorization/ScaffoldPermissionCommandTest.php`.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — the signature:**
      ```
      permissions:scaffold
          {module   : the owning module, e.g. Procurement}
          {resource : kebab-case resource, e.g. purchase-orders}
          {verb     : a PermissionVerb case value, e.g. approve}
          {--qualifier=  : snake_case narrowing}
          {--template=*  : template keys receiving it by default (admin is implicit and refused)}
          {--sod-group=}
          {--dry-run     : print the five diffs; write nothing}
      ```
- [ ] **Step 2 — the command, in full.** Rev 2 supplied the signature, the five rules and an ordered summary of `handle()`; plan gate r2 **B2-5** rejects that as dispatch-ready, because the AST-free source rewriting and the sorting are the hard part and an implementer would have had to design them. The body below implements rules 1–5 exactly as the table states them.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Shared\Domain\Authorization\InvalidPermissionKey;
use App\Shared\Domain\Authorization\PermissionDefinition;
use App\Shared\Domain\Authorization\PermissionVerb;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Filesystem\Filesystem;

/**
 * The deterministic five-artifact generator behind contract AI-1: one manifest
 * entry, one enum case, three locale stubs.
 *
 * DETERMINISM is the whole contract (Task 1.15's five rules):
 *   1. the manifest entry goes at a FIXED ANCHOR and the list is re-sorted by
 *      (resource, action) — never appended at the cursor;
 *   2. the enum case goes before the class's closing brace and the case block is
 *      re-sorted by case name;
 *   3. JSON is written with PRETTY_PRINT|UNESCAPED_SLASHES|UNESCAPED_UNICODE,
 *      ksort(..., SORT_STRING), exactly one trailing newline;
 *   4. every write is atomic: render to <path>.scaffold.tmp, then rename();
 *   5. a second identical invocation writes NOTHING and exits 0.
 *
 * It REFUSES rather than guessing when the module has no manifest or no enum.
 */
final class ScaffoldPermission extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'permissions:scaffold
        {module   : the owning module, e.g. Procurement}
        {resource : kebab-case resource, e.g. purchase-orders}
        {verb     : a PermissionVerb case value, e.g. approve}
        {--qualifier=  : snake_case narrowing}
        {--template=*  : template keys receiving it by default (admin is implicit and refused)}
        {--sod-group=}
        {--dry-run     : print the five diffs; write nothing}';

    protected $description = 'Scaffold one permission across its manifest, its module enum and the three locale bundles.';

    private const LOCALES = ['en', 'fr', 'ar'];

    /** Rule 3, in one place so the label skeleton command can state the same contract. */
    private const JSON_FLAGS = JSON_PRETTY_PRINT | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE;

    public function __construct(private readonly Filesystem $files)
    {
        parent::__construct();
    }

    public function handle(): int
    {
        $module = (string) $this->argument('module');
        $resource = (string) $this->argument('resource');
        $verbValue = (string) $this->argument('verb');

        $qualifierOption = $this->option('qualifier');
        $qualifier = is_string($qualifierOption) && $qualifierOption !== '' ? $qualifierOption : null;

        $sodOption = $this->option('sod-group');
        $sodGroup = is_string($sodOption) && $sodOption !== '' ? $sodOption : null;

        /** @var list<string> $templates */
        $templates = array_values(array_map(strval(...), (array) $this->option('template')));

        // (1) the verb, through the CLOSED enum.
        $verb = PermissionVerb::tryFrom($verbValue);
        if ($verb === null) {
            $this->error(sprintf(
                "'%s' is not a PermissionVerb. The closed set is: %s",
                $verbValue,
                implode(', ', array_map(static fn (PermissionVerb $v): string => $v->value, PermissionVerb::cases())),
            ));

            return self::FAILURE;
        }

        // (2)+(3) construct through make(), so a malformed resource, a malformed
        // qualifier or an explicit `admin` template throws make()'s OWN message.
        // The command never re-implements the validation.
        try {
            $definition = PermissionDefinition::make(
                module: $module,
                resource: $resource,
                verb: $verb,
                qualifier: $qualifier,
                templateDefaults: $templates,
                sodGroup: $sodGroup,
            );
        } catch (InvalidPermissionKey $invalid) {
            $this->error($invalid->getMessage());

            return self::FAILURE;
        }

        $key = $definition->key;

        // (4) locate the two files, or REFUSE with the three exact steps.
        $manifestPath = base_path(sprintf('app/Modules/%s/Domain/Authorization/%sPermissionManifest.php', $module, $module));
        $enumPath = base_path(sprintf('app/Modules/%s/Domain/Enums/%sPermission.php', $module, $module));

        if (! $this->files->exists($manifestPath) || ! $this->files->exists($enumPath)) {
            $this->error(sprintf('%s has no permission manifest or no permission enum yet. Three things to do, in this order:', $module));
            $this->line(sprintf('  1. create app/Modules/%s/Domain/Authorization/%sPermissionManifest.php implementing PermissionManifest', $module, $module));
            $this->line(sprintf('  2. create app/Modules/%s/Domain/Enums/%sPermission.php as a string-backed enum', $module, $module));
            $this->line(sprintf("  3. add \$this->app->tag([%sPermissionManifest::class], 'permission.manifests'); to %s's service provider register()", $module, $module));

            return self::FAILURE;
        }

        $manifestSource = $this->files->get($manifestPath);

        // (5) IDEMPOTENCE: already declared is a no-op success.
        if (str_contains($manifestSource, "'".$key."'")) {
            $this->info(sprintf('already declared: %s — nothing written.', $key));

            return self::SUCCESS;
        }

        // (6) render the five artifacts. Each renderer is pure: source in, source out.
        $artifacts = [
            $manifestPath => $this->renderManifest($manifestSource, $definition, $module, $resource, $verb, $qualifier, $templates, $sodGroup),
            $enumPath => $this->renderEnum($this->files->get($enumPath), $key),
        ];
        foreach (self::LOCALES as $locale) {
            $localePath = base_path(sprintf('../web/src/locales/%s/common.json', $locale));
            $artifacts[$localePath] = $this->renderLocale($this->files->get($localePath), $module, $verb->value);
        }

        $dryRun = (bool) $this->option('dry-run');

        foreach ($artifacts as $path => $contents) {
            if ($dryRun) {
                $this->line('--- '.$path);
                $this->line($this->diff($this->files->get($path), $contents));

                continue;
            }
            $this->writeAtomically($path, $contents);
        }

        // (7)
        $this->info(($dryRun ? 'would write' : 'wrote').' five artifacts for '.$key.':');
        foreach (array_keys($artifacts) as $path) {
            $this->line('  '.$path);
        }
        $this->warn('Now run: php artisan permissions:export-frontend-map');

        return self::SUCCESS;
    }

    /**
     * RULE 1. Insert at the fixed anchor `    return [` inside definitions(),
     * then re-sort the whole block by (resource, action) and re-emit. Two
     * invocations in either order produce the same file.
     *
     * @param  list<string>  $templates
     */
    private function renderManifest(
        string $source,
        PermissionDefinition $definition,
        string $module,
        string $resource,
        PermissionVerb $verb,
        ?string $qualifier,
        array $templates,
        ?string $sodGroup,
    ): string {
        $lines = explode("\n", $source);

        $anchor = null;
        foreach ($lines as $index => $line) {
            if (preg_match('/^\s*return \[$/', $line) === 1) {
                $anchor = $index;
                break;
            }
        }
        if ($anchor === null) {
            throw new \RuntimeException('manifest has no `return [` anchor inside definitions(); it is not a generated-shape manifest.');
        }

        $close = null;
        for ($i = $anchor + 1, $n = count($lines); $i < $n; $i++) {
            if (preg_match('/^\s*\];$/', $lines[$i]) === 1) {
                $close = $i;
                break;
            }
        }
        if ($close === null) {
            throw new \RuntimeException('manifest has no `];` closing the definitions() list.');
        }

        $body = array_slice($lines, $anchor + 1, $close - $anchor - 1);
        $body = array_values(array_filter($body, static fn (string $line): bool => trim($line) !== ''));
        $body[] = $this->manifestLine($module, $resource, $verb, $qualifier, $templates, $sodGroup);

        // Deterministic order: (resource, action), read back off each line.
        usort($body, fn (string $a, string $b): int => $this->manifestSortKey($a) <=> $this->manifestSortKey($b));

        return implode("\n", [
            ...array_slice($lines, 0, $anchor + 1),
            ...$body,
            ...array_slice($lines, $close),
        ]);
    }

    /** @param list<string> $templates */
    private function manifestLine(string $module, string $resource, PermissionVerb $verb, ?string $qualifier, array $templates, ?string $sodGroup): string
    {
        $arguments = [
            $this->quote($module),
            $this->quote($resource),
            'PermissionVerb::'.$verb->name,
        ];
        if ($qualifier !== null) {
            $arguments[] = 'qualifier: '.$this->quote($qualifier);
        }
        if ($templates !== []) {
            $arguments[] = 'templateDefaults: ['.implode(', ', array_map($this->quote(...), $templates)).']';
        }
        if ($sodGroup !== null) {
            $arguments[] = 'sodGroup: '.$this->quote($sodGroup);
        }

        return '            PermissionDefinition::make('.implode(', ', $arguments).'),';
    }

    /**
     * The sort key of one manifest line: [resource, action]. Three shapes exist
     * in the catalogue and all three are matched here —
     *   PermissionDefinition::make('Mod', 'resource', PermissionVerb::Case, ...)
     *   PermissionDefinition::legacy('resource.action', 'Mod', ...)
     *   PermissionDefinition::deprecate(PermissionDefinition::make(...), ...)
     * — the third by the same `make` pattern, because the inner call is present.
     *
     * @return array{0: string, 1: string}
     */
    private function manifestSortKey(string $line): array
    {
        if (preg_match("/make\(\s*'[^']+',\s*'([^']+)',\s*PermissionVerb::(\w+)/", $line, $m) === 1) {
            $verb = constant(PermissionVerb::class.'::'.$m[2]);
            $action = $verb instanceof PermissionVerb ? $verb->value : strtolower($m[2]);
            if (preg_match("/qualifier:\s*'([^']+)'/", $line, $q) === 1) {
                $action .= '_'.$q[1];
            }

            return [$m[1], $action];
        }

        if (preg_match("/legacy\(\s*'([^']+)'/", $line, $m) === 1) {
            $position = strrpos($m[1], '.');

            return $position === false ? [$m[1], ''] : [substr($m[1], 0, $position), substr($m[1], $position + 1)];
        }

        // An unrecognised line sorts last and keeps its relative order, so a
        // hand-written comment inside the list is never silently reordered away.
        return ['~~~', $line];
    }

    /** RULE 2. Append before the LAST `}` line, then re-sort the contiguous case block by case name. */
    private function renderEnum(string $source, string $key): string
    {
        $lines = explode("\n", $source);

        $closing = null;
        for ($i = count($lines) - 1; $i >= 0; $i--) {
            if (preg_match('/^\}$/', $lines[$i]) === 1) {
                $closing = $i;
                break;
            }
        }
        if ($closing === null) {
            throw new \RuntimeException('enum file has no closing brace on its own line.');
        }

        $first = null;
        $last = null;
        foreach ($lines as $index => $line) {
            if (preg_match('/^\s*case \w+ = /', $line) === 1) {
                $first ??= $index;
                $last = $index;
            }
        }

        $newCase = sprintf('    case %s = %s;', $this->caseName($key), $this->quote($key));

        if ($first === null || $last === null) {
            return implode("\n", [...array_slice($lines, 0, $closing), $newCase, ...array_slice($lines, $closing)]);
        }

        $cases = array_slice($lines, $first, $last - $first + 1);
        $cases[] = $newCase;
        sort($cases, SORT_STRING);

        return implode("\n", [...array_slice($lines, 0, $first), ...$cases, ...array_slice($lines, $last + 1)]);
    }

    /** Case name = PascalCase of the WHOLE key, splitting on `.`, `-` and `_`. Collision-free by construction. */
    private function caseName(string $key): string
    {
        $parts = preg_split('/[.\-_]+/', $key) ?: [];

        return implode('', array_map(static fn (string $part): string => ucfirst(strtolower($part)), $parts));
    }

    /** RULE 3. Add the two label stubs if absent, ksort both blocks, one trailing newline. */
    private function renderLocale(string $source, string $module, string $action): string
    {
        /** @var array<string, mixed> $bundle */
        $bundle = json_decode($source, true, 512, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);

        /** @var array<string, mixed> $permissions */
        $permissions = is_array($bundle['permissions'] ?? null) ? $bundle['permissions'] : [];
        /** @var array<string, string> $modules */
        $modules = is_array($permissions['modules'] ?? null) ? $permissions['modules'] : [];
        /** @var array<string, string> $actions */
        $actions = is_array($permissions['actions'] ?? null) ? $permissions['actions'] : [];

        $modules[$module] ??= $module;
        $actions[$action] ??= $action;

        ksort($modules, SORT_STRING);
        ksort($actions, SORT_STRING);

        $permissions['modules'] = $modules;
        $permissions['actions'] = $actions;
        ksort($permissions, SORT_STRING);
        $bundle['permissions'] = $permissions;

        return json_encode($bundle, self::JSON_FLAGS | JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR)."\n";
    }

    /** RULE 4. A generator interrupted halfway must not corrupt a 300-key locale bundle. */
    private function writeAtomically(string $path, string $contents): void
    {
        $temporary = $path.'.scaffold.tmp';
        $this->files->put($temporary, $contents);
        if (! rename($temporary, $path)) {
            $this->files->delete($temporary);

            throw new \RuntimeException('could not atomically replace '.$path);
        }
    }

    /** A minimal unified-diff renderer for --dry-run. Line-oriented; no external dependency. */
    private function diff(string $before, string $after): string
    {
        $old = explode("\n", $before);
        $new = explode("\n", $after);
        $out = [];
        foreach (array_diff($new, $old) as $line) {
            $out[] = '+'.$line;
        }
        foreach (array_diff($old, $new) as $line) {
            $out[] = '-'.$line;
        }

        return $out === [] ? '  (no change)' : implode("\n", $out);
    }

    private function quote(string $value): string
    {
        return "'".str_replace(['\\', "'"], ['\\\\', "\\'"], $value)."'";
    }
}
~~~

This is the case that makes determinism checkable;
